using System;
using Beam.Serialization.Json;
using Beam.Utilities;
using Funlabs;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Crafting;

public class Plant : MonoBehaviour, ISaveable
{
	[SerializeField]
	private Transform _fruitPosition;

	[SerializeField]
	private GameObject[] _plantStages;

	[SerializeField]
	private GameObject _deadStage;

	private PlantModel _plantModel;

	private PlantFruit _plantFruit;

	private PlantType _plantType;

	private float _growthTime;

	public PlantType PlantType => _plantType;

	public float GrowthTime => _growthTime;

	public void Initialize(IFruitFactory fruitFactory, PlantType plantType, float growthTime)
	{
		_plantType = plantType;
		_growthTime = growthTime;
		_plantModel = new PlantModel(_plantStages, _deadStage, _growthTime);
		_plantFruit = new PlantFruit(fruitFactory, _plantType, _fruitPosition, _growthTime);
	}

	public void Reset()
	{
		_plantModel.StartedGrowingTime = GameTime.Now;
		_plantFruit.LastHarvestedTime = GameTime.Now;
		_plantModel.Dead = false;
		_plantModel.UpdateStage();
	}

	public void Poll()
	{
		_plantModel.Poll();
		if (!_plantModel.Dead)
		{
			_plantFruit.Poll();
		}
	}

	public void Kill()
	{
		_plantFruit.ClearFruit(spoil: true);
		_plantModel.Dead = true;
		_plantModel.UpdateStage();
	}

	public void ClearFruit()
	{
		_plantFruit.ClearFruit();
	}

	public JObject Save()
	{
		JObject jObject = new JObject();
		jObject.AddField("StartedGrowingTime", JSerializer.Serialize(_plantModel.StartedGrowingTime));
		jObject.AddField("LastHarvestedTime", JSerializer.Serialize(_plantFruit.LastHarvestedTime));
		JObject jObject2 = new JObject();
		if (!_plantFruit.Fruit.IsNullOrDestroyed())
		{
			jObject2.AddField("Fruit", _plantFruit.Fruit.ReferenceId.ToString());
			jObject2.AddField("isGrowing", _plantFruit.Fruit.IsGrowing);
		}
		jObject.AddField("Fruits", jObject2);
		return jObject;
	}

	public void Load(JObject data)
	{
		_plantModel.StartedGrowingTime = JSerializer.Deserialize<DateTime>(data.GetField("StartedGrowingTime"));
		_plantFruit.LastHarvestedTime = JSerializer.Deserialize<DateTime>(data.GetField("LastHarvestedTime"));
		JObject fruitsData = data.GetField("Fruits");
		if (fruitsData != null && !fruitsData.IsNull())
		{
			LevelLoader.CurrentLoader.DeferredLoading.Add(() =>
			{
				MiniGuid referenceId = fruitsData.GetField("Fruit").GetValue<string>().ToMiniGuid();
				bool value = fruitsData.GetField("isGrowing").GetValue<bool>();
				InteractiveObject_FOOD interactiveObject_FOOD = MultiplayerMng.FindObject<InteractiveObject_FOOD>(referenceId);
				_plantFruit.AddFruit(interactiveObject_FOOD, interactiveObject_FOOD.transform);
				_plantFruit.UpdateFruitGrowing(value);
			});
		}
		else
		{
			_plantFruit.CheckFruit();
		}
		_plantModel.Poll();
	}
}

SOURCE FILE 152
PATH: Assembly-CSharp__Beam.Crafting__PlantFruit.cs

using System;
using Beam.Utilities;
using Funlabs;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Crafting;

public class PlantFruit
{
	private readonly IFruitFactory _fruitFactory;

	private readonly PlantType _plantType;

	private readonly float _growthTime;

	private readonly Transform _fruitPosition;

	private InteractiveObject_FOOD _fruit;

	private DateTime _lastHarvestedTime;

	public InteractiveObject_FOOD Fruit => _fruit;

	public DateTime LastHarvestedTime { get; set; }

	public PlantFruit(IFruitFactory fruitFactory, PlantType plantType, Transform fruitPosition, float growthTime)
	{
		_fruitFactory = fruitFactory;
		_plantType = plantType;
		_fruitPosition = fruitPosition;
		_growthTime = growthTime;
	}

	public void Poll()
	{
		CheckFruit();
	}

	public void CheckFruit()
	{
		if (_fruit != null)
		{
			_fruit.IsGrowing = true;
		}
		else if (GameTime.GetHoursSince(LastHarvestedTime) > _growthTime)
		{
			CreateFruit();
		}
	}

	private void CreateFruit()
	{
		if (!Game.Mode.IsClient())
		{
			InteractiveObject_FOOD interactiveObject_FOOD = _fruitFactory.CreateFruit(_plantType);
			interactiveObject_FOOD.transform.position = _fruitPosition.position;
			interactiveObject_FOOD.transform.rotation = _fruitPosition.rotation;
			interactiveObject_FOOD.IsGrowing = true;
			AddFruit(interactiveObject_FOOD, _fruitPosition);
		}
	}

	public void AddFruit(InteractiveObject_FOOD fruit, Transform position)
	{
		_fruit = fruit;
		_fruit.Attach(new StaticAttachingAnchor(position));
		_fruit.DetachedEvent += Fruit_Detached;
		fruit.IsGrowing = true;
	}

	public void ClearFruit(bool spoil = false)
	{
		if (!(_fruit == null))
		{
			_fruit.IsGrowing = false;
			_fruit.DetachedEvent -= Fruit_Detached;
			_fruit.DetachFromImpact();
			if (spoil)
			{
				_fruit.Spoil();
			}
			if (_fruit.IsDamageable)
			{
				BeamTeam.Destroy(_fruit.gameObject);
			}
			_fruit = null;
		}
	}

	private void Fruit_Detached(IAttachable sender)
	{
		if (sender as InteractiveObject_FOOD != null)
		{
			LastHarvestedTime = GameTime.Now;
			_fruit.IsGrowing = false;
			_fruit = null;
		}
	}

	public void UpdateFruitGrowing(bool isGrowing)
	{
		_fruit.IsGrowing = isGrowing;
	}
}

PATH: Assembly-CSharp__Beam.Crafting__Plot.cs

SHA256: eed463ea4f0de28fa0b5d7840469ed3ac27a72c6bdac3f980917342c7f7b24b8

using System;
using Beam.Serialization;
using Beam.Serialization.Json;
using Beam.Utilities;
using Funlabs;
using Photon.Bolt;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Crafting;

[RequireComponent(typeof(Farming_PLOT))]
public class Plot : MultiplayerBehaviour, IMultiplayerState<IPlotState>
{
	public class ReplicateKillCrop : MultiplayerMessage
	{
		[Replicate]
		public SaveablePrefab Prefab;

		public override void OnPeer()
		{
			Prefab.GetComponent<Plot>().KillCrop();
		}
	}

	public class ReplicatePoll : MultiplayerMessage
	{
		[Replicate]
		public SaveablePrefab Prefab;

		public override void OnPeer()
		{
			Prefab.GetComponent<Plot>().PollPlant_InvokeRepeating();
		}
	}

	private const float DEPLETE_INTERVAL_HOURS = 24f;

	[SerializeField]
	private int _maxWater = 4;

	[SerializeField]
	private Transform _plantPosition;

	private IPlantFactory _plantFactory;

	private Plant _plant;

	private bool _growing;

	private bool _isRaining;

	private int _water = 1;

	public bool Growing => _growing;

	public IPlotState MultiplayerState => GetMultiplayerState<IPlotState>();

	public event Action<PlantType> CropCreated;

	public bool HasPlants()
	{
		return _plant != null;
	}

	public float GetNormalizedWater()
	{
		return (float)_water / (float)_maxWater;
	}

	protected virtual void OnCropCreated(PlantType type)
	{
		CropCreated?.Invoke(type);
	}

	private void Awake()
	{
		FarmingManager plantFactory = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<FarmingManager>();
		_plantFactory = plantFactory;
	}

	private void Start()
	{
		if (!Game.Mode.IsClient() && Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance != null)
		{
			Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance.WeatherStarted += AtmosphereStorm_WeatherStarted;
			Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance.WeatherStopped += AtmosphereStorm_WeatherStopped;
			if (Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance.CurrentWeatherEvent != null)
			{
				AtmosphereStorm_WeatherStarted(Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance.CurrentWeatherEvent);
			}
		}
	}

	private void OnDestroy()
	{
		CropCreated = null;
		if (!Game.Mode.IsClient() && Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance != null)
		{
			Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance.WeatherStarted -= AtmosphereStorm_WeatherStarted;
			Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance.WeatherStopped -= AtmosphereStorm_WeatherStopped;
		}
	}

	private void AtmosphereStorm_WeatherStarted(WeatherEvent weather)
	{
		if (Mathf.Approximately(weather.Humidity, 100f))
		{
			_isRaining = true;
			int num = 1;
			InvokeRepeating("PollRain_InvokeRepeating", GameTime.OneGameHour, (float)num * GameTime.OneGameHour);
		}
	}

	private void AtmosphereStorm_WeatherStopped()
	{
		_isRaining = false;
		CancelInvoke("PollRain_InvokeRepeating");
	}

	public void Place()
	{
	}

	public bool Plant(Plantable plantable)
	{
		if (_growing)
		{
			return false;
		}
		ClearCrop();
		if (_water < 1)
		{
			_water = 1;
		}
		CreateCrop(plantable.PlantType);
		Initialize();
		return true;
	}

	private void CreateCrop(PlantType plantType)
	{
		_growing = true;
		_plant = _plantFactory.CreatePlant(plantType);
		_plant.transform.parent = gameObject.transform;
		_plant.transform.position = _plantPosition.position;
		OnCropCreated(plantType);
	}

	private void Initialize()
	{
		if (Game.Mode.IsMaster())
		{
			CancelInvoke("PollWater_InvokeRepeating");
			CancelInvoke("PollPlant_InvokeRepeating");
			InvokeRepeating("PollWater_InvokeRepeating", 24f * GameTime.OneGameHour, 24f * GameTime.OneGameHour);
			InvokeRepeating("PollPlant_InvokeRepeating", 3f, GameTime.OneGameHour);
		}
	}

	private void PollRain_InvokeRepeating()
	{
		if (!LevelLoader.IsLoading(gameObject) && !LevelLoader.IsServerJoinInProgress && _isRaining)
		{
			_water++;
			_water = Mathf.Clamp(_water, 0, _maxWater);
		}
	}

	private void PollPlant_InvokeRepeating()
	{
		if (!LevelLoader.IsLoading() && !LevelLoader.IsServerJoinInProgress && _growing && !(_plant == null))
		{
			if (Game.Mode.IsServer())
			{
				ReplicatePoll replicatePoll = new ReplicatePoll();
				replicatePoll.Prefab = GetComponent<SaveablePrefab>();
				replicatePoll.Post();
			}
			_plant.Poll();
		}
	}

	private void PollWater_InvokeRepeating()
	{
		if (!LevelLoader.IsLoading() && !LevelLoader.IsServerJoinInProgress && !_isRaining)
		{
			_water--;
			_water = Mathf.Clamp(_water, 0, _maxWater);
			if (_water <= 0)
			{
				ReplicatedKillCrop();
			}
		}
	}

	public bool CanWaterCrop()
	{
		if (_growing)
		{
			return _water < _maxWater;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool WaterCrop(int amount)
	{
		if (_growing && _water < _maxWater)
		{
			_water += amount;
			_water = Mathf.Clamp(_water, 0, _maxWater);
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool ClearCrop()
	{
		if (_plant == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		_plant.ClearFruit();
		_plantFactory.DestroyPlant(_plant);
		_plant = null;
		_growing = false;
		if (Game.Mode.IsMaster())
		{
			CancelInvoke("PollWater_InvokeRepeating");
		}
		return true;
	}

	private void KillCrop()
	{
		if (_growing)
		{
			_plant.Kill();
			_water = 0;
			_growing = false;
			if (Game.Mode.IsMaster())
			{
				CancelInvoke("PollWater_InvokeRepeating");
			}
		}
	}

	protected void FixedUpdate()
	{
		if (Game.Mode.IsMultiplayer())
		{
			if (!entity.IsOwner)
			{
				_water = MultiplayerState.Water;
				_growing = MultiplayerState.Growing;
			}
			else
			{
				MultiplayerState.Water = _water;
				MultiplayerState.Growing = _growing;
			}
		}
	}

	private void ReplicatedKillCrop()
	{
		SaveablePrefab component = GetComponent<SaveablePrefab>();
		if (component != null && component.IsOwner)
		{
			ReplicateKillCrop replicateKillCrop = new ReplicateKillCrop();
			replicateKillCrop.Prefab = GetComponent<SaveablePrefab>();
			replicateKillCrop.Post();
		}
		KillCrop();
	}

	public JObject Save()
	{
		JObject jObject = new JObject();
		jObject.AddField("Water", _water);
		PlantType plantType = ((_plant != null) ? _plant.PlantType : PlantType.NONE);
		jObject.AddField("PlantType", JSerializer.Serialize(plantType));
		if (_plant == null)
		{
			return jObject;
		}
		JObject jObject2 = new JObject();
		jObject2.Add(SavingUtilities.Save(_plant));
		jObject.AddField("Plants", jObject2);
		jObject.AddField("LastPollTime", JSerializer.Serialize(GameTime.Now));
		return jObject;
	}

	public void Load(JObject data)
	{
		JObject field = data.GetField("Water");
		if ((bool)field)
		{
			_water = field.GetValue<int>();
		}
		PlantType plantType = JSerializer.Deserialize<PlantType>(data.GetField("PlantType"));
		if (plantType == PlantType.NONE)
		{
			return;
		}
		CreateCrop(plantType);
		if (_water <= 0)
		{
			ReplicatedKillCrop();
			return;
		}
		JObject data2 = data.GetField("Plants")?.Children.FirstOrDefault_NonAlloc() ?? data.GetField("[0]Plant");
		if (SavingUtilities.Load(_plant, data2))
		{
			JObject field2 = data.GetField("LastPollTime");
			if (field2 != null && !field2.IsNull())
			{
				int num = Mathf.RoundToInt((float)(int)GameTime.GetHoursSince(JSerializer.Deserialize<DateTime>(field2)) / 24f);
				_water -= num;
				if (_water <= 0)
				{
					ReplicatedKillCrop();
					return;
				}
			}
			Initialize();
		}
		else
		{
			ClearCrop();
		}
	}
}

using Beam.Serialization.Json;
using Beam.Utilities;
using Funlabs;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Crafting;

public class ProjectileObject : ConstructionObject, IPickupable, IAnimatablePrimary, ICraftingDisplayableStorable, IDisplayableStorable, IStorable, IBase, IHasCraftingType, ISaveablePrefab, ISaveableReference, ISaveable, ICraftingMaterial, IDraggable, IPhysical, ISelectable, IProjectile
{
	[PREFAB_PROCESSOR_GetComponent]
	[SerializeField]
	private ProjectileMovement _projectileMovement;

	[SerializeField]
	private BaseObjectProxy _proxy;

	private float _mass;

	private float _drag;

	private float _angularDrag;

	private RigidbodyInterpolation _interpolation;

	private CollisionDetectionMode _collision;

	public ProjectileMovement ProjectileMovement => _projectileMovement;

	public BaseObjectProxy Proxy => _proxy;

	protected override void Awake()
	{
		base.Awake();
		_projectileMovement.Initialize(this);
		_projectileMovement.enabled = false;
		CacheRigidbodyParameters();
		if (!LevelLoader.IsLoading())
		{
			rigidbody.useGravity = true;
		}
	}

	private void CacheRigidbodyParameters()
	{
		_mass = rigidbody.mass;
		_drag = rigidbody.drag;
		_angularDrag = rigidbody.angularDrag;
		_interpolation = rigidbody.interpolation;
		_collision = rigidbody.collisionDetectionMode;
	}

	public override void Attached()
	{
		base.Attached();
		rigidbody.isKinematic = !IsOwner;
	}

	protected override void OnPlayerDisconnect(PlayerDisconnectedEvent disconnectedEvent)
	{
		bool flag = !Owner.IsNullOrDestroyed();
		base.OnPlayerDisconnect(disconnectedEvent);
		if (!flag && !_projectileMovement.Thrower.IsNullOrDestroyed() && _projectileMovement.Thrower.Id == disconnectedEvent.PlayerId)
		{
			MultiplayerMng.Destroy(this);
		}
	}

	public override bool Store()
	{
		Reset();
		return base.Store();
	}

	protected override void OnTransformParentChanged()
	{
		base.OnTransformParentChanged();
		if (!rigidbody.IsNullOrDestroyed())
		{
			rigidbody.interpolation = ((ParentId != TransformId.Unknown) ? _interpolation : RigidbodyInterpolation.None);
		}
	}

	public virtual void Reset()
	{
		colliders.EnableColliders(enabled: true, isTrigger: false);
		this.CheckRigidbody(IsOwner);
		rigidbody.mass = _mass;
		rigidbody.drag = _drag;
		rigidbody.angularDrag = _angularDrag;
		rigidbody.interpolation = _interpolation;
		rigidbody.collisionDetectionMode = _collision;
		_projectileMovement.Reset();
		_projectileMovement.enabled = false;
		CanParent = true;
		CanDrag = true;
	}

	public virtual void Pierce(ProjectileHit hit)
	{
		Transform transform = hit.Transform;
		if (hit.Object.IsValid() && hit.Transform.gameObject.layer != Layers.PROJECTILE_HITBOXES)
		{
			transform = hit.Object.transform;
		}
		if (transform.CompareTag("Terrain"))
		{
			this.SetKinematic(kinematic: true);
			CanDrag = false;
			return;
		}
		bool isTrigger = hit.Object.IsValid() && hit.Object.transform.CompareTag("Shark");
		colliders.EnableColliders(enabled: true, isTrigger);
		this.RemoveRigidbody(IsOwner, immediate: true);
		if (transform != null)
		{
			this.ParentTo(transform);
		}
		CanParent = false;
		CanDrag = false;
	}

	public virtual void LocalFaunaPierce()
	{
		colliders.EnableColliders(enabled: true, isTrigger: true);
		this.RemoveRigidbody(IsOwner, immediate: true);
		CanParent = false;
		CanDrag = false;
	}

	public override JObject Save()
	{
		JObject jObject = base.Save();
		if (!_projectileMovement.AttachedBase.IsNullOrDestroyed())
		{
			ISaveablePrefab saveablePrefab = _projectileMovement.AttachedBase as ISaveablePrefab;
			if (!saveablePrefab.IsNullOrDestroyed())
			{
				JObject jObject2 = new JObject();
				jObject2.AddField("AttachedId", saveablePrefab.ReferenceId.ToString());
				jObject2.AddField("Parent", transform.parent.name);
				jObject.AddField("ProjectileAttached", jObject2);
			}
		}
		return jObject;
	}

	public override void Load(JObject data)
	{
		base.Load(data);
		CacheRigidbodyParameters();
		JObject attachedData = data.GetField("ProjectileAttached");
		if (!attachedData)
		{
			return;
		}
		LevelLoader.CurrentLoader.DeferredLoading.Add(() =>
		{
			JObject field = attachedData.GetField("AttachedId");
			BaseObject baseObject = MultiplayerMng.FindObject<BaseObject>(field.GetValue<string>().ToMiniGuid());
			if (baseObject != null)
			{
				_projectileMovement.AttachedBase = baseObject;
				JObject field2 = attachedData.GetField("Parent");
				Transform transform = baseObject.transform.FindNestedChild(field2.GetValue<string>());
				ProjectileHit hit = new ProjectileHit(transform, baseObject);
				Pierce(hit);
				JSerializer.DeserializeInto(data.GetField("Transform"), base.transform);
				CompleteTransformSerialization();
			}
			else
			{
				Debug.LogError($"Projectile:: Couldn't find attached [{field}]");
				MultiplayerMng.Destroy(this, DestroyDelay.LATE);
			}
		});
	}
}

using Beam.Events;
using Beam.Utilities;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Crafting;

public class QuickAccessSlotUnlocker : BaseObject, ICraftable, IBase, IHasCraftingType, IPlaceable
{
	[SerializeField]
	private int _toolbeltLevel;

	[SerializeField]
	private string _nextCombinationName;

	[SerializeField]
	private InteracterUnityEvent _crafted;

	public int CreatorId { get; set; }

	public bool CraftImmediate => true;

	public float PlacingDistance { get; }

	public GhostCollider.ColliderType ColliderType { get; }

	public Mesh GhostMesh { get; }

	public Renderer GhostRenderer { get; }

	public Material[] GhostMaterials => GhostRenderer.sharedMaterials;

	protected override void Awake()
	{
		base.Awake();
		_crafted.AddListener(UnlockSlot);
	}

	private void UnlockSlot(IPlayer player)
	{
		EventManager.RaiseEvent(new QuickAccessSlotUnlockedEvent(CreatorId, _toolbeltLevel));
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_nextCombinationName))
		{
			player.Crafter.CraftingCombinations.UnlockCombination(_nextCombinationName);
		}
		BeamTeam.Destroy(gameObject);
	}

	public void Place()
	{
	}

	public bool OwnerPlacing(Transform ghost, BeamRay ray, float yRotation)
	{
		return false;
	}

	public bool ReplicantPlacing(Transform ghost, BeamRay ray, float yRotation)
	{
		return false;
	}

	public bool CheckProximity(Transform ghost)
	{
		return false;
	}

	public void OnBeginCrafting()
	{
	}

	public void OnCrafted(IPlayer player)
	{
		_crafted?.Invoke(player);
	}
}

SOURCE FILE 158
PATH: Assembly-CSharp__Beam.Intro__AircraftAssetLoader.cs

using System.Collections;
using Beam.Serialization;
using Beam.Serialization.Json;
using Beam.Utilities;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Intro;

public class AircraftAssetLoader : SaveablePrefab
{
	[SerializeField]
	private string _assetBundleName;

	[SerializeField]
	private string _assetName;

	private Coroutine _loadSequence;

	private AircraftManager _aircraftManager;

	public override void OnCustomMapLoaded()
	{
		base.OnCustomMapLoaded();
		LoadAssets(null);
	}

	public Coroutine LoadAssets(JObject data)
	{
		_loadSequence = StartCoroutine(LoadAssets_Sequence(data));
		return _loadSequence;
	}

	private void LoadingAssetsFailed()
	{
		Debug.LogError("EndGameAssetLoader::LoadingAssetsFailed:: [" + gameObject.name + "] failed to load! Unloading and destroying any loaded assets.");
		UnloadAndDestroyAssets();
	}

	private void CreateAsset(GameObject prefab)
	{
		if (!(prefab == null))
		{
			GameObject gameObject = Object.Instantiate(prefab);
			_aircraftManager = gameObject.GetComponent<AircraftManager>();
			_aircraftManager.transform.SetParent(transform, worldPositionStays: false);
		}
	}

	public void UnloadAndDestroyAssets()
	{
		this.SafeStopCoroutine(_loadSequence);
		_loadSequence = null;
		if (!_aircraftManager.IsNullOrDestroyed())
		{
			BeamTeam.Destroy(_aircraftManager.gameObject);
		}
		Singleton<AssetBundleManager>.Instance?.UnloadAssetBundle(_assetBundleName);
	}

	private void OnDestroy()
	{
		UnloadAndDestroyAssets();
	}

	public override JObject Save()
	{
		JObject jObject = base.Save();
		jObject.AddField("Transform", JSerializer.Serialize(transform));
		JObject data = SavingUtilities.Save(_aircraftManager);
		jObject.AddField("EndGameManager", data);
		return jObject;
	}

	public override void Load(JObject data)
	{
		base.Load(data);
		transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
		CompleteTransformSerialization();
		JObject field = data.GetField("EndGameManager");
		if (field != null && !field.IsNull())
		{
			LoadAssets(field);
		}
	}

	private IEnumerator LoadAssets_Sequence(JObject data = null)
	{
		yield return Singleton<AssetBundleManager>.Instance.LoadAssetAsync<GameObject>(_assetName, _assetBundleName, CreateAsset, LoadingAssetsFailed);
		if (data != null)
		{
			yield return 0f;
			yield return 0f;
			SimpleLoader simpleLoader = new SimpleLoader();
			simpleLoader.IsLoading = true;
			LevelLoader.CurrentLoader = simpleLoader;
			_aircraftManager.Load(data);
			for (int i = 0; i < simpleLoader.DeferredLoading.Count; i++)
			{
				LoadingActionUtilities.ExecuteDeferredAction(simpleLoader.DeferredLoading[i]);
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < simpleLoader.PostLoading.Count; j++)
			{
				LoadingActionUtilities.ExecuteDeferredAction(simpleLoader.PostLoading[j]);
			}
			for (int k = 0; k < simpleLoader.PhysicalLoading.Count; k++)
			{
				LoadingActionUtilities.ExecuteDeferredAction(simpleLoader.PhysicalLoading[k]);
			}
			simpleLoader.IsLoading = false;
			LevelLoader.CurrentLoader = null;
		}
	}
}

using Beam.Utilities;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;
using UnityEngine.Serialization;

namespace Beam.Intro;

public class AircraftProximity : MonoBehaviour
{
	[SerializeField]
	private bool _debug;

	private const float PROXIMITY_HEIGHT = 8f;

	private const float PROXIMITY_DISTANCE = 10f;

	[FormerlySerializedAs("_position")]
	[SerializeField]
	private Transform _proximityPosition;

	private bool _inProximity;

	[Header("EVENTS")]
	public UnityEvent EnteredProximity;

	public UnityEvent LeftProximity;

	private void Start()
	{
		InvokeRepeating("Update_InvokeRepeating", 0f, 1f);
	}

	private void Update_InvokeRepeating()
	{
		if (LevelLoader.IsLoading() || LevelLoader.IsServerJoinInProgress)
		{
			return;
		}
		IPlayer player = PlayerUtilities.FindClosestPlayer(_proximityPosition.position);
		if (!player.IsNullOrDestroyed())
		{
			bool flag = CheckBounds(player.transform.position);
			if (flag && !_inProximity)
			{
				EnterProximity();
			}
			else if (!flag && _inProximity)
			{
				LeaveProximity();
			}
		}
	}

	private void EnterProximity()
	{
		EnteredProximity?.Invoke();
		_inProximity = true;
	}

	private void LeaveProximity()
	{
		LeftProximity?.Invoke();
		_inProximity = false;
	}

	private bool CheckBounds(Vector3 position)
	{
		if (Vector3Tools.SqrDistanceOnAxis(_proximityPosition.position, position, Vector3Tools.xz) < BeamMath.Sqr(10f) && position.y > _proximityPosition.position.y)
		{
			return position.y < _proximityPosition.position.y + 8f;
		}
		return false;
	}
}

	}

	private IPlayer GetClosestPlayer(Vector3 position)
	{
		IPlayer result = PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers[0];
		if (Game.Mode.GetPlayersCount() == 1)
		{
			return result;
		}
		float num = float.PositiveInfinity;
		for (int i = 0; i < PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers.Count; i++)
		{
			IPlayer player = PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers[i];
			float magnitude = (position - player.transform.position).magnitude;
			if (magnitude < num)
			{
				num = magnitude;
				result = player;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void EnterHangar()
	{
		if (_enteredHangar)
		{
			PlayTrack(TrackType.Ambience_Carrier);

	private IEnumerator<float> EnterHangar_Sequence()
	{
		SpawnGyroRewardPieces();
		IPlayer closestPlayer = GetClosestPlayer(_enterHangarPosition.position);
		closestPlayer.Character.PlayingEventAnimation = true;
		SetCharacterPosition(closestPlayer, _enterHangarPosition);
		PlayCharacterAnimation(closestPlayer, "EndGame_EnterHangar");
		FreezePlayer(closestPlayer);
		closestPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetRotation(closestPlayer.Character.CharacterFirstPerson.CameraRigPosition);
		closestPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetCameraTarget(closestPlayer.Character.CharacterFirstPerson.CameraRigPosition);
		closestPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = false;
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_enterHangarClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(8f);
		closestPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ResetCameraTarget();
		closestPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetPosition(closestPlayer.Character.CharacterFirstPerson.CameraRigPosition);
		closestPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = closestPlayer.IsOwner;
		SetCharacterPosition(closestPlayer, _endHangarPosition);
		closestPlayer.Character.PlayingEventAnimation = false;
		ResetCharacter(closestPlayer);
		PlayTrack(TrackType.Ambience_Carrier);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_enterHangarSequence);
	}

	private void SpawnGyroRewardPieces()

		if (!Game.Mode.IsClient())

			uint num = Prefabs.ConvertLegacyPrefabId(_gyroPartID);
			if (num == 0)
			{
				Debug.LogError("AircraftVFX :: SpawnGyroRewardPieces : Missing Gyrocopter Reward Piece!");

			for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
			{
				MultiplayerMng.Instantiate<SaveablePrefab>(num, _gyroRewardPiecePositions[i].position, _gyroRewardPiecePositions[i].rotation, MiniGuid.New());
			}
		}
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> ObjectivesCompleted_Sequence(AircraftObjectivesCompletedArgs args)

		ActivateParts();

				_player1Speech.Play("STATUS_END_OBJECTIVES_COMPLETED", _player1.Gender, underwater: false);

	private void EnterCockpit()
	{
		if (Game.Mode.IsMultiplayer())
		{
			MultiplayerMng.UpdateSessionToken_IsBusy(isBusy: true);
		}
		if (_player1.IsOwner)
		{
			_player1.PlayerCamera.SetCameraMode(CameraViewMode.FIRST_PERSON);
		}
		if (_player2 != null && _player2.IsOwner)
		{
			_player2.PlayerCamera.SetCameraMode(CameraViewMode.FIRST_PERSON);
		}
		_enterCockpitSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(EnterCockpit_Sequence());
	}

	public void ReplicatedEnterCockpit()
	{
		if (Game.Mode.IsMultiplayer())
		{
			ReplicateEnterCockpit replicateEnterCockpit = new ReplicateEnterCockpit();
			replicateEnterCockpit.aircraftVFX = this;
			replicateEnterCockpit.Post();
		}
		EnterCockpit();
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> EnterCockpit_Sequence()
	{
		EventManager.RaiseEvent(new EndGameSequenceEvent());
		EventManager.RaiseEvent(new FreezeNotificationsEvent(freeze: true));
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			if (allPlayer.IsOwner)
			{
				allPlayer.Input.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: false, 0);
				allPlayer.Input.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: true, 10);
			}
			allPlayer.Character.HideCurrentItem();
			FreezePlayer(allPlayer);
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetRotation(allPlayer.Character.CharacterFirstPerson.CameraRigPosition);
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetCameraTarget(allPlayer.Character.CharacterFirstPerson.CameraRigPosition);
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = false;
		}
		PlayTrack(TrackType.Ambience_Cockpit);
		_aircraftStation.SetActive(value: false);
		float num = Mathf.Abs(Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.MilitaryTime - 16f);
		if (Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.MilitaryTime > 16f)
		{
			num = 24f - num;
		}
		float num2 = num * 60f * 60f;
		float rate = num2 / 3f / (float)GameTime.TIME_SCALE;
		while (Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.MilitaryTime > 16f)
		{
			Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.Tick += Time.deltaTime * rate;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		while (Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.MilitaryTime < 16f)
		{
			Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.Tick += Time.deltaTime * rate;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		while (_ambientLight.intensity > 0f)
		{
			_ambientLight.intensity -= Time.deltaTime;
			if (_ambientLight.intensity <= 0f)
			{
				_ambientLight.enabled = false;
			}
			yield return 0f;
		}
		Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.MilitaryTime_NoEvents = 16f;
		Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.Paused = true;
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		_player1.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ResetCameraTarget();
		_player1.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = _player1.IsOwner;
		if (_player2 != null)
		{
			_player2.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ResetCameraTarget();
			_player2.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = _player2.IsOwner;
		}
		_tightCameraClamp = true;
		ClampCameras();
		_player1.Character.PlayingEventAnimation = true;
		SetCharacterPosition(_player1, _cockpitOperatingPosition);
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "EndGame_EnterCockpit");
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_Enter");
		if (_player2 != null)
		{
			_player2.Character.PlayingEventAnimation = true;
			SetCharacterPosition(_player2, _backseatOperatingPosition);
			PlayCharacterAnimation(_player2, "EndGame_EnterCockpit_2");
		}
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_enterCockpitClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		_aircraftManager.Objectives.CompleteFinalObjective();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(8f);
		SetCharacterPosition(_player1, _cockpitOperatingPosition);
		_player1.Character.transform.parent = _cockpitOperatingPosition;
		_player1.Character.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
		if (_player2 != null)
		{
			SetCharacterPosition(_player2, _backseatOperatingPosition);
			_player2.Character.transform.parent = _backseatOperatingPosition;
			_player2.Character.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		_tightCameraClamp = false;
		_player1.Statistics.Invincible = true;
		Sunburn playerEffect = _player1.Statistics.GetPlayerEffect<Sunburn>();
		if (playerEffect != null)
		{
			playerEffect.ResetExposure();
		}
		if (_player2 != null)
		{
			_player2.Statistics.Invincible = true;
			Sunburn playerEffect2 = _player2.Statistics.GetPlayerEffect<Sunburn>();
			if (playerEffect2 != null)
			{
				playerEffect2.ResetExposure();
			}
		}
		InteractionCheck(InteractionSequence.CheckSystems);
	}

	public void InteractionCheck(InteractionSequence sequence)
	{
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_interactionSequence);
		switch (sequence)
		{
		case InteractionSequence.CheckSystems:
			_interactionSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(InteractionCheck_Sequence());
			break;
		case InteractionSequence.StartEngine:
			_interactionSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(InteractionStartEngine_Sequence());
			break;
		case InteractionSequence.CloseCockpit:
			_interactionSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(InteractionCloseCockpit_Sequence());
			break;
		case InteractionSequence.EngageRail:
			_interactionSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(InteractionRail_Sequence());
			break;
		case InteractionSequence.EngageEngine:
			_interactionSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(InteractionEngageEngine_Sequence());
			break;
		case InteractionSequence.TakeOff:
			_interactionSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(InteractionLaunch_Sequence());
			break;
		}
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> InteractionCheck_Sequence()
	{
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "Cockpit_Check");
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_Check");
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.8f);
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_cockpitCheckClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(10f);
		_cockpitInteraction.SetActive(value: true);
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> InteractionStartEngine_Sequence()
	{
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "Cockpit_StartEngine");
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_StartEngine");
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_cockpitStartEngineClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		_cockpitLights.SetActive(value: true);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(3.2f);
		_maxPropellerSpeed = 0.3f;
		RotatePropellers();
		CameraShakeEffect(0.001f, -1f);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(11.1f);
		_idleEngineAudio.Play();
		_cockpitInteraction.SetActive(value: true);
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> InteractionCloseCockpit_Sequence()
	{
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "Cockpit_Close");
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_Close");
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_cockpitCloseClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(3.5f);
		_idleEngineAudioFilter.enabled = true;
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(6.1f);
		CloseEngineHatch();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(2.5f);
		_cockpitInteraction.SetActive(value: true);
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> InteractionRail_Sequence()
	{
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "Cockpit_Rail");
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_Rail");
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_cockpitRailClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(4.5f);
		Quaternion startingRotation = _aircraftRail.localRotation;
		Quaternion endRotation = Quaternion.identity;
		float timer = 0f;
		while (timer < 9f)
		{
			float t = timer / 9f;
			_aircraftRail.localRotation = Quaternion.Lerp(startingRotation, endRotation, t);
			timer += Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		_aircraftRail.localRotation = endRotation;
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		_cockpitInteraction.SetActive(value: true);
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> InteractionEngageEngine_Sequence()
	{
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "Cockpit_EngageEngine");
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_Throttle");
		RaiseRail();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(3f);
		CameraShakeEffect(0.005f, 60f);
		_maxPropellerSpeed = 1f;
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(2f);
		_idleEngineAudio.Stop();
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_cockpitThrottleClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(2f);
		_throttledEngineAudio.Play();
		Quaternion startingRotation = _aircraft.localRotation;
		Quaternion endRotation = Quaternion.identity;
		float timer = 0f;
		while (timer < 3f)
		{
			float t = timer / 3f;
			_aircraft.localRotation = Quaternion.Lerp(startingRotation, endRotation, t);
			timer += Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		_aircraft.localRotation = endRotation;
		_cockpitInteraction.SetActive(value: true);
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> InteractionLaunch_Sequence()
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			if (allPlayer.IsOwner)
			{
				allPlayer.Input.DisableMaps();
				allPlayer.Input.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: true, 9);
			}
		}
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "Cockpit_Launch");
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_Launch");
		PlayTrackInstantly(TrackType.Cockpit_TakeOff);
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_cockpitLaunchClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1.5f);
		_aircraft.transform.parent = _aircraftTakeOffAnimation.transform;
		float num = 1f;
		float animationTime = _aircraftTakeOffAnimation["Aircraft_TakeOff"].length * (1f / num);
		_aircraftTakeOffAnimation["Aircraft_TakeOff"].speed = num;
		_aircraftTakeOffAnimation.Play();
		CameraSpeedEffect(90f, 1f);
		CameraShakeEffect(0.01f, 60f);
		float timer = 0f;
		bool launched = false;
		while (timer < animationTime)
		{
			if (timer > 7f && !launched)
			{
				launched = true;
				FlyAircraft();
			}
			timer += Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_interactionSequence);
	}

	public void FlyAircraft()
	{
		_flyingSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(FlyAircraft_Sequence());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> FlyAircraft_Sequence()
	{
		CameraSpeedEffect(60f, 3f);
		float flightTime = 25f;
		float roll = 0f;
		CameraSpeedEffect(60f, 3f);
		CameraShakeEffect(0.01f, flightTime + 3f);
		float timer = 0f;
		while (timer < flightTime)
		{
			if (_player1.IsOwner)
			{
				float axis = _player1.Input.GetAxis(0);
				roll += axis;
				roll = Mathf.Clamp(roll, -10f, 10f);
			}
			_aircraft.localRotation = Quaternion.Lerp(_aircraft.localRotation, Quaternion.Euler(new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0f - roll)), Time.deltaTime);
			timer += Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		CrashAircraft();
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_flyingSequence);
	}

	public void CrashAircraft()
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			if (allPlayer.IsOwner)
			{
				allPlayer.Movement.IsInEndGameScene = true;
			}
		}
		_crashSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(CrashAircraft_Sequence());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> CrashAircraft_Sequence()
	{
		Singleton<Fader>.Instance?.SetFadeColor(Color.black);
		Singleton<Fader>.Instance?.FadeOut(3f);
		while (_throttledEngineAudio.volume > 0f)
		{
			_throttledEngineAudio.volume -= 0.1f * Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(5f);
		TMPTextScreenViewAdapter textView = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<TMPTextScreenViewAdapter>();
		textView?.SetHeaderText("AIRCRAFT_TEXT_SCREEN_LABEL_THREE_HOURS_LATER");
		textView?.SetSubheaderText("");
		textView?.SetTooltipText("");
		textView.Show();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(4f);
		yield return _introAssetLoader.Load_Async();
		_introAssetLoader.Jet.SetupEndingScene();
		textView.Hide();
		SetupCrashScene();
		Singleton<Fader>.Instance?.FadeIn(0.1f);
		_throttledEngineAudio.volume = 1f;
		CameraShakeEffect(0.001f, 60f);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		if (Game.Mode.IsMaster() && _player1.IsOwner)
		{
			EventManager.RaiseEvent(new CompletedEndGameEvent());
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(15f);
		_tightCameraClamp = true;
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(3f);
		_crashPrivateJetTrans.gameObject.SetActive(value: true);
		float num = 1f;
		float animationTime = _crashAnimation["Aircraft_Crash"].length * (1f / num);
		_crashAnimation["Aircraft_Crash"].speed = num;
		_crashAnimation.Play();
		PlayAircraftAnimation("Cockpit_Crash");
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(2f);
		PlayCharacterAnimation(_player1, "Cockpit_Crash");
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_cockpitCrashClip, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		PlayTrackInstantly(TrackType.Cockpit_Crash);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(animationTime - 3f);
		CameraSpeedEffect(90f, 0.1f);
		_crashImpact.SetActive(value: true);
		_throttledEngineAudio.Stop();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.1f);
		_tightCameraClamp = false;
		_crashImpact.SetActive(value: false);
		yield return _introAssetLoader.Jet.EndingSequence();
		Crashed();
		_introAssetLoader.IntroAssets.SetActive(value: false);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_rotatePropeller);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_clampCamera);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_crashSequence);
	}

	private void Crashed()
	{
		Timing.RunCoroutine(Crashed_Sequence());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> Crashed_Sequence()
	{
		UVideoScreenViewAdapter video = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<UVideoScreenViewAdapter>();
		video?.Show();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(12f);
		yield return Singleton<Fader>.Instance.FadeOut_Async(7f);
		video?.Hide();
		UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<EndGameCreditsController>().Show();
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			if (allPlayer.IsOwner)
			{
				allPlayer.Movement.IsInEndGameScene = false;
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetupCrashScene()
	{
		transform.parent.transform.parent = null;
		Singleton<StrandedWorld>.Instance?.gameObject.SetActive(value: false);
		Ceto.Ocean.Instance.gameObject.SetActive(value: false);
		GameObject.Find("Manager - Terrain Impostors").SetActive(value: false);
		Singleton<GameTime>.Instance.MilitaryTime = 21f;
		EventManager.RaiseEvent(new EndGameSequenceEvent());
		_crashAnimation.gameObject.SetActive(value: true);
		_crashAircraftTrans.gameObject.SetActive(value: true);
		_aircraft.transform.parent = _crashAircraftTrans;
		_aircraft.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
		_aircraft.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
	}

	private void RotatePropellers()
	{
		_rotatePropeller = Timing.RunCoroutine(RotatePropeller());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> RotatePropeller()
	{
		while (true)
		{
			if (_curPropellerSpeed < _maxPropellerSpeed)
			{
				_curPropellerSpeed += 0.1f * Time.deltaTime;
			}
			float num = 500f * _curPropellerSpeed * Time.deltaTime;
			_propeller.transform.Rotate(0f, 0f, 0f - num);
			yield return 0f;
		}
	}

	private void ClampCameras()
	{
		_player1.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ResetCameraTarget();
		if (_player2 != null)
		{
			_player2.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ResetCameraTarget();
		}
		_clampCamera = Timing.RunCoroutine(ClampCameraUpdate());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> ClampCameraUpdate()
	{
		while (true)
		{
			ClampCamera(_player1, _player1.Character.CharacterFirstPerson.CameraRigPosition);
			if (_player2 != null)
			{
				ClampCamera(_player2, _player2.Character.CharacterFirstPerson.CameraRigPosition);
			}
			yield return 0f;
		}
	}

	private void ClampCamera(IPlayer player, Transform target)
	{
		if (_tightCameraClamp)
		{
			player.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.ClampViewToPosUpdate(target.forward, 10f, 10f, lerpAngles: true);
		}
		else
		{
			player.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.ClampViewToPosUpdate(target.forward, 60f, 45f);
		}
	}

	private void ActivateParts()
	{
		_enginePart.SetActive(value: true);
		_propellerPart.SetActive(value: true);
	}

	private void CloseEngineHatch()
	{
		_trolley.SetActive(value: false);
		float z = _engingeHatch.localRotation.z;
		LeanTween.value(gameObject, RotateEngineHatch, z, 0f, 3f).setEase(LeanTweenType.easeInOutCubic);
	}

	private void RotateEngineHatch(float rotation)
	{
		_engingeHatch.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(_engingeHatch.localRotation.x, _engingeHatch.localRotation.y, rotation);
	}

	private void CloseFuelHatches()
	{
		float x = _fuelHatch1.localRotation.x;
		LeanTween.value(gameObject, RotateFuelHatch, x, 0f, 1f).setEase(LeanTweenType.easeInOutCubic);
	}

	private void RotateFuelHatch(float rotation)
	{
		_fuelHatch1.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(rotation, _fuelHatch1.localRotation.y, _fuelHatch1.localRotation.z);
		_fuelHatch2.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(rotation, _fuelHatch2.localRotation.y, _fuelHatch2.localRotation.z);
	}

	private void RaiseRail()
	{
		_ = _rail.localRotation;
		LeanTween.value(gameObject, RotateRail, 0f, 5f, 2f).setEase(LeanTweenType.easeInOutCubic);
	}

	private void RotateRail(float rotation)
	{
		_rail.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(rotation, 0f, 0f);
	}

	private void SkipToLaunch()
	{
		_player1.Character.PlayingEventAnimation = true;
		SetPlayer2();
		_throttledEngineAudio.Play();
		_cockpitLights.SetActive(value: true);
		SetCharacterPosition(_player1, _cockpitOperatingPosition);
		_player1.Character.transform.parent = _cockpitOperatingPosition;
		_player1.Character.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
		_maxPropellerSpeed = 2f;
		RotatePropellers();
		CloseEngineHatch();
		ActivateParts();
		_aircraftRail.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
		_aircraft.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
		RaiseRail();
		InteractionCheck(InteractionSequence.TakeOff);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_enterCockpitSequence);
	}

	private void SkipToCrash()
	{
		CrashAircraft();
	}

	public void PlayTrackInstantly(TrackType track)
	{
		EventManager.RaiseEvent(new TrackEvent(track, TrackEventType.Play_Instantly));
	}

	public void PlayTrack(TrackType track)
	{
		EventManager.RaiseEvent(new TrackEvent(track, TrackEventType.Play));
	}

	public void StopTrack(TrackType track)
	{
		EventManager.RaiseEvent(new TrackEvent(track, TrackEventType.Stop));
	}

	public new JObject Save()
	{
		JObject jObject = new JObject();
		jObject.AddField("Entered", _enteredHangar);
		jObject.AddField("EnteredHangar", _enteredHangar);
		jObject.AddField("Completed", _completed);
		return jObject;
	}

	public new void Load(JObject data)
	{
		if (data.GetField("EnteredHangar") != null)
		{
			_enteredHangar = data.GetField("EnteredHangar").GetValue<bool>();
		}
		_completed = data.GetField("Completed").GetValue<bool>();
	}
}

using System;
using Beam.Crafting;
using Beam.Events;
using Beam.Language;
using Beam.UI;
using Funlabs;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;

namespace Beam.Intro;

public class Interactive_AIRCRAFT : ReplicableMonoBehaviour, IInteractable, IBase, IHasCraftingType, ISelectable
{
	[SerializeField]
	[LocalizationTermDropdown]
	[LocalizationTermPreview]
	private string _displayName;

	private MiniGuid _referenceId;

	[PREFAB_PROCESSOR_GetComponent]
	[SerializeField]
	private Selecting _selecting;

	public UnityEvent Interacted;

	public BoolUnityEvent Activated;

	private float CHARACTER_MIN_DISTANCE = 3f;

	public string DisplayName => _displayName;

	public override MiniGuid ReferenceId
	{
		get
		{
			if (_referenceId.IsDefault())
			{
				_referenceId = MiniGuid.INTERACTIVE_AIRCRAFT;
			}
			return _referenceId;
		}
		set
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	public Selecting Selecting => _selecting;

	public Transform TransformParent { get; set; }

	public CraftingType CraftingType { get; } = CraftingType.Empty;

	public RaycastMode RaycastMode { get; }

	public bool CanParent { get; set; }

	public bool IsDamageable { get; }

	public bool CanInteract { get; set; } = true;

	public string GetInteractionDescription(int playerId, IBase obj)
	{
		return "AIRCRAFT_INTERACTION_DESCRIPTION_GO_HOME";
	}

	private void Start()
	{
		EventManager.AddListener<PlayerDisconnectedEvent>(EventManager_PlayerDisconnected);
	}

	private void OnDestroy()
	{
		EventManager.RemoveListener<PlayerDisconnectedEvent>(EventManager_PlayerDisconnected);
	}

	public void Damage(float damage, IBase damager)
	{
	}

	public void Destroy()
	{
	}

	public void Parent()
	{
	}

	public void Activate(bool activate)
	{
		Activated?.Invoke(activate);
	}

	public bool Interact(IPlayer player)
	{
		if (Game.Mode.IsClient() || !player.IsOwner)
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_CLIENT_INTERACT");
			return false;
		}
		DisplayDialogue(player);
		return false;
	}

	public bool InteractWithObject(IPlayer player, IBase obj)
	{
		return Interact(player);
	}

	private bool ValidateRequirements(IPlayer player)
	{
		WeatherEvent weatherEvent = Singleton<AtmosphereStorm>.Instance?.CurrentWeatherEvent;
		if (weatherEvent != null && weatherEvent.HasStarted && Mathf.Approximately(weatherEvent.Humidity, 100f))
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_WAIT_WEATHER_DESC");
			return false;
		}
		if (player.Statistics.HasStatusEffect(StatusEffect.STARVATION))
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_EAT_BEFORE_LEAVE_DESC");
			return false;
		}
		if (player.Statistics.HasStatusEffect(StatusEffect.DEHYDRATION))
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_DRINK_BEFORE_LEAVE_DESC");
			return false;
		}
		if (player.Statistics.HasStatusEffect(StatusEffect.BROKEN_BONES))
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_FIX_BROKEN_BONES_DESC");
			return false;
		}
		if (player.Statistics.HasStatusEffect(StatusEffect.BLEEDING))
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_FIX_BLEEDING_DESC");
			return false;
		}
		if (player.Statistics.HasStatusEffect(StatusEffect.POISON))
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_CURE_POISON_DESC");
			return false;
		}
		if ((Game.Mode.IsMultiplayer() || Game.Mode.IsLocalCoop()) && !CheckAllPlayersInRange(player))
		{
			LocalizedNotification.Post(player, NotificationPriority.Immediate, 4f, "AIRCRAFT_NTF_CANT_LEAVE_DESC");
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	private bool CheckAllPlayersInRange(IPlayer player)
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			if ((player.transform.position - allPlayer.transform.position).magnitude > CHARACTER_MIN_DISTANCE)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private void DisplayDialogue(IPlayer player)
	{
		ShowDialogue(player);
		DialogueBoxViewAdapter dialogueBoxViewAdapter = DialogueBox.Show(player.Input, "AIRCRAFT_DB_GO_HOME_TITLE", "AIRCRAFT_DB_GO_HOME_DESC", "AIRCRAFT_DB_BUTTON_YES", "AIRCRAFT_DB_BUTTON_SAVE", "AIRCRAFT_DB_BUTTON_NO_SCARED");
		dialogueBoxViewAdapter.Accept += () =>
		{
			HideDialogue(player);
			if (ValidateRequirements(player))
			{
				Interacted?.Invoke();
			}
		};
		dialogueBoxViewAdapter.Decline += () =>
		{
			SaveGame(player);
			HideDialogue(player);
		};
		dialogueBoxViewAdapter.Cancel += () =>
		{
			HideDialogue(player);
		};
	}

	private void ShowDialogue(IPlayer player)
	{
		RewiredUtilities.Players.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: false, 0);
		player.Input.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: true, 16);
		player.Input.SetCursorEnabled(enabled: true);
	}

	private void HideDialogue(IPlayer player)
	{
		RewiredUtilities.Players.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: true, 0);
		player.Input.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: false, 16);
		player.Input.SetCursorEnabled(enabled: false);
	}

	private void SaveGame(IPlayer player)
	{
		HideDialogue(player);
		SaveManager.SaveGame(player);
	}

	private void EventManager_PlayerDisconnected(PlayerDisconnectedEvent disconnectedEvent)
	{
		RewiredUtilities.Players.SetAndCacheMapsEnabled(state: false, 10);
	}
}

using System;
using Beam.Crafting;
using Funlabs;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Intro;

public class Interactive_AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT : ReplicableMonoBehaviour, IInteractable, IBase, IHasCraftingType, ISelectable
{
	private MiniGuid _referenceId;

	[PREFAB_PROCESSOR_GetComponent]
	[SerializeField]
	private Selecting _selecting;

	private int _currentStage = 1;

	[Header("EVENTS")]
	public InteractionSequenceEvent Interacted;

	public override MiniGuid ReferenceId
	{
		get
		{
			if (_referenceId.IsDefault())
			{
				_referenceId = MiniGuid.INTERACTIVE_AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT;
			}
			return _referenceId;
		}
		set
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	public Selecting Selecting => _selecting;

	public Transform TransformParent { get; set; }

	public string DisplayName { get; protected set; } = "";

	public CraftingType CraftingType { get; } = CraftingType.Empty;

	public RaycastMode RaycastMode { get; }

	public bool CanParent { get; set; }

	public bool IsDamageable { get; }

	public bool CanInteract { get; set; } = true;

	public string GetInteractionDescription(int playerId, IBase obj)
	{
		if (Game.Mode.IsClient())
		{
			return string.Empty;
		}
		string result = "";
		if (_currentStage == 0)
		{
			result = "AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT_INTERACTION_DESCRIPTION_CHECK_SYSTEMS";
		}
		if (_currentStage == 1)
		{
			result = "AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT_INTERACTION_DESCRIPTION_START_ENGINE";
		}
		if (_currentStage == 2)
		{
			result = "AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT_INTERACTION_DESCRIPTION_CLOSE_COCKPIT";
		}
		if (_currentStage == 3)
		{
			result = "AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT_INTERACTION_DESCRIPTION_ENGAGE_RAIL";
		}
		if (_currentStage == 4)
		{
			result = "AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT_INTERACTION_DESCRIPTION_THROTTLE_ENGINE";
		}
		if (_currentStage == 5)
		{
			result = "AIRCRAFT_COCKPIT_INTERACTION_DESCRIPTION_LAUNCH";
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void Start()
	{
		DisplayName = string.Empty;
		CanInteract = true;
	}

	public void Damage(float damage, IBase damager)
	{
	}

	public void Destroy()
	{
	}

	public void Parent()
	{
	}

	public void Activate(bool toggle)
	{
		CanInteract = toggle;
	}

	public bool Interact(IPlayer player)
	{
		if ((Game.Mode.IsClient() && player.IsOwner) || (Game.Mode.IsServer() && !player.IsOwner))
		{
			return false;
		}
		Interacted?.Invoke((InteractionSequence)_currentStage);
		_currentStage++;
		if (Game.Mode.IsMaster())
		{
			gameObject.SetActive(value: false);
		}
		return false;
	}

	public bool InteractWithObject(IPlayer player, IBase obj)
	{
		return Interact(player);
	}
}

using System;
using Beam.Crafting;
using Beam.Language;
using Beam.Serialization.Json;
using Funlabs;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;

namespace Beam.Intro;

public class Interactive_AIRCRAFT_STATION : ReplicableMonoBehaviour, IInteractable, IBase, IHasCraftingType, ISelectable, IHaveAttribute, IHaveFuel
{
	[SerializeField]
	[LocalizationTermDropdown]
	[LocalizationTermPreview]
	private string _displayName;

	[PREFAB_PROCESSOR_GetComponent]
	[SerializeField]
	private Selecting _selecting;

	[Header("ATTRIBUTE")]
	[SerializeField]
	private IconType _type;

	private MiniGuid _referenceId;

	[Header("REFILLING VALUES")]
	[SerializeField]
	private float _maxValue = 1f;

	[SerializeField]
	private float _value = 0.7f;

	[SerializeField]
	private RefillerBase _refiller;

	[Header("STATION OBJECTIVE")]
	[SerializeField]
	private int _endGameObjectiveId = -1;

	private AircraftManager _aircraftManager;

	[Header("EVENTS")]
	public BoolUnityEvent Activated;

	public UnityEvent Filled;

	public string DisplayName => _displayName;

	public Selecting Selecting => _selecting;

	public IconType Type => _type;

	public Transform TransformParent { get; set; }

	public CraftingType CraftingType => CraftingType.Empty;

	public RaycastMode RaycastMode => RaycastMode.Collider;

	public bool CanParent { get; set; }

	public bool IsDamageable => false;

	public bool CanInteract { get; set; } = true;

	public override MiniGuid ReferenceId
	{
		get
		{
			if (_referenceId.IsDefault())
			{
				_referenceId = MiniGuid.AIRCRAFT_STATION(transform.position);
			}
			return _referenceId;
		}
		set
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	float IHaveFuel.FuelCapacity => _maxValue;

	float IHaveFuel.Fuel => _value;

	public float GetValue()
	{
		return _value / _maxValue;
	}

	public string GetInteractionDescription(int playerId, IBase obj)
	{
		return _refiller.GetInteractionDescription(this, obj);
	}

	protected virtual void OnFilled()
	{
		CanInteract = false;
		Filled?.Invoke();
	}

	public void Initialize(AircraftManager aircraftManager)
	{
		_aircraftManager = aircraftManager;
		_aircraftManager.Proximity.EnteredProximity.AddListener(EndGameProximity_EnteredProximity);
		_aircraftManager.Proximity.LeftProximity.AddListener(EndGameProximity_LeftProximity);
		_aircraftManager.Objectives.ObjectivesCompleted.AddListener(EndGameObjectives_ObjectivesCompleted);
	}

	private void OnDestroy()
	{
		if (_aircraftManager != null)
		{
			_aircraftManager.Proximity?.EnteredProximity.RemoveListener(EndGameProximity_EnteredProximity);
			_aircraftManager.Proximity?.LeftProximity.RemoveListener(EndGameProximity_LeftProximity);
			_aircraftManager.Objectives?.ObjectivesCompleted.RemoveListener(EndGameObjectives_ObjectivesCompleted);
		}
	}

	public void Damage(float damage, IBase damager)
	{
	}

	public void Destroy()
	{
	}

	public void Parent()
	{
	}

	private void EndGameObjectives_ObjectivesCompleted(AircraftObjectivesCompletedArgs args)
	{
		Activated?.Invoke(arg0: false);
	}

	private void EndGameProximity_EnteredProximity()
	{
		Activated?.Invoke(arg0: true);
	}

	private void EndGameProximity_LeftProximity()
	{
		Activated?.Invoke(arg0: false);
	}

	public bool Interact(IPlayer player)
	{
		return false;
	}

	public bool InteractWithObject(IPlayer player, IBase obj)
	{
		return _refiller.DoRefill(new RefillArgs
		{
			Player = player,
			Refillable = this,
			Obj = obj
		});
	}

	float IHaveFuel.Refill(float availableFuel)
	{
		float num = Mathf.Min(_maxValue - _value, availableFuel);
		if (num > 0f)
		{
			_value += num;
			_value = Mathf.Clamp(_value, 0f, _maxValue);
			if (_value == _maxValue)
			{
				_aircraftManager.Objectives.CompleteObjective(_endGameObjectiveId);
				OnFilled();
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	public override JObject Save()
	{
		JObject jObject = new JObject();
		jObject.AddField("Value", _value);
		return jObject;
	}

	public override void Load(JObject data)
	{
		_value = data.GetField("Value").GetValue<float>();
		if (_value == _maxValue)
		{
			OnFilled();
		}
	}
}

	private void PauseMusic(MainMenuOpenedEvent e)
	{
		if (e.Opened)
		{
			_jetExplosion.Pause();
		}
		else if (!e.Opened)
		{
			_jetExplosion.UnPause();
		}
	}

	private void SetupCharacterDummy()
	{
		bool flag = PlayerUtilities.GetServerCharacterGender() == EGameGenderMode.Male;
		_maleDummy.SetActive(flag);
		_femaleDummy.SetActive(!flag);
		if (Game.Mode.IsSinglePlayer())
		{
			_maleDummy2.SetActive(value: false);
			_femaleDummy2.SetActive(value: false);
		}
		else
		{
			if ((!Game.Mode.IsMultiplayer() && !Game.Mode.IsLocalCoop()) || PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers.Count <= 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag2 = PlayerUtilities.GetClientCharacterGender() == EGameGenderMode.Male;
			_maleDummy2.SetActive(flag2);
			_femaleDummy2.SetActive(!flag2);
			GameObject gameObject = (flag2 ? _maleDummy2 : _femaleDummy2);
			gameObject.transform.position = _MpCharacterPos.position;
			if (Game.Mode.IsMultiplayer())
			{
				if (Game.Mode.IsServer())
				{
					gameObject = ((PlayerUtilities.GetClientCharacterGender() == EGameGenderMode.Female) ? _femaleDummy2 : _maleDummy2);
				}
				else
				{
					gameObject = ((PlayerUtilities.GetServerCharacterGender() == EGameGenderMode.Female) ? _femaleDummy : _maleDummy);
				}
				Transform transform = gameObject.transform.FindInChildren("head");
				if (transform != null)
				{
					transform.gameObject.SetActive(value: true);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!Game.Mode.IsLocalCoop())
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (IPlayer player in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
				{
					player.PlayerCamera.RenderingCameraChanged += (bool ourCamera) =>
					{
						if (player.Id == 0)
						{
							_maleDummy2.transform.FindInChildren("head").gameObject.SetActive(ourCamera);
							_femaleDummy2.transform.FindInChildren("head").gameObject.SetActive(ourCamera);
						}
						else
						{
							_maleDummy.transform.FindInChildren("head").gameObject.SetActive(ourCamera);
							_femaleDummy.transform.FindInChildren("head").gameObject.SetActive(ourCamera);
						}
					};
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private IEnumerator Start()
	{
		MainMenuPresenter mainMenuPresenter = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<MainMenuPresenter>();
		if ((bool)mainMenuPresenter)
		{
			mainMenuPresenter.QuittingToMenu += MainMenuPresenter_QuittingToMenu;
		}
		EventManager.AddListener<MainMenuOpenedEvent>(PauseMusic);
		MultiplayerMng.AddListener<GameStateChangeGlobalEvent>(Listener_GameStateChangeGlobalEvent);
		_sceneContainer = transform.parent.gameObject;
		_JetAmbience.outputAudioMixerGroup = ((AudioManager)AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer()).GetAudioMixerGroup(AudioMixerChannels.Environment);
		while (!LevelLoader.IsPlayable())
		{
			yield return null;
		}
		SetupControls();
		SetupScene();
		SetupCharacterDummy();
		if (Game.State != GameState.INTRO)
		{
			yield break;
		}
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			SeatingPositionData seatingPositionData = _seatingPositionsData[allPlayer.Id];
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.transform.position = seatingPositionData.PlayerControllerPosition.position;
		}
		SetupPlayers();
		_wakeUpSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(WakeUp());
	}

	private void OnDestroy()
	{
		MainMenuPresenter mainMenuPresenter = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<MainMenuPresenter>();
		if ((bool)mainMenuPresenter)
		{
			mainMenuPresenter.QuittingToMenu -= MainMenuPresenter_QuittingToMenu;
		}
		EventManager.RemoveListener<MainMenuOpenedEvent>(PauseMusic);
		MultiplayerMng.RemoveListener<GameStateChangeGlobalEvent>(Listener_GameStateChangeGlobalEvent);
	}

	private void SetupPlayers()
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer.Movement.IsBusy = default;
			allPlayer.Movement.IntroSceneSetup();
			allPlayer.Statistics.Invincible = true;
			SeatingPositionData seatingPositionData = _seatingPositionsData[allPlayer.Id];
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetCameraTarget(seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget);
			allPlayer.transform.localScale = allPlayer.transform.localScale * 0.1f;
			Vector3 position = seatingPositionData.PlayerControllerPosition.position + new Vector3(0f, 0f, 10f);
			allPlayer.transform.position = position;
			allPlayer.transform.SetAsFirstSibling();
		}
	}

	private void SetupControls()
	{
		CursorUtils.Visible = false;
		CursorUtils.LockState = CursorLockMode.Locked;
		_originalPixelLights = QualitySettings.pixelLightCount;
		QualitySettings.pixelLightCount = 10;
	}

	private void Update()
	{
		if (LevelLoader.IsLoadingScene())
		{
			return;
		}
		Rewired.Player player = ReInput.players.GetPlayer(0);
		if (player == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (_time)
		{
			_timer += Time.deltaTime;
			if (_timer > 0.25f || !player.GetButton(39))
			{
				_time = false;
				if (_timer < 0.25f)
				{
					if (_canPause)
					{
						MainMenuPresenter mainMenuPresenter = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<MainMenuPresenter>();
						if ((bool)mainMenuPresenter && !Singleton<Fader>.Instance.IsFading)
						{
							mainMenuPresenter.Show(player);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (_canSkipIntro && !LevelLoader.IsLoading())
				{
					LoadNewGame();
				}
				return;
			}
		}
		if (LevelLoader.IsPlayable() && Game.State == GameState.INTRO && player.GetButtonDown(39) && _canSkipIntro)
		{
			_time = true;
			_timer = 0f;
		}
	}

	private void SetupScene()
	{
		_jetEngine.pitch = 0.75f;
		_backLights.SetActive(value: false);
		_audioSource = GetComponent<AudioSource>();
	}

	public void SetupEndingScene()
	{
		_jetDamaged.SetActive(value: true);
		_jetComplete.SetActive(value: false);
	}

	public float EndingSequence()
	{
		return Timing.WaitUntilDone(Timing.RunCoroutine(EndGameSequence()));
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> EndGameSequence()
	{
		_JetAmbience.Play();
		SetupPlayers();
		_audioSource.PlayOneShot(_beltWarning);
		if (PlayerUtilities.GetServerCharacterGender() == EGameGenderMode.Male)
		{
			_audioSource.PlayOneShot(_wakeAudio);
		}
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			SeatingPositionData seatingPositionData = _seatingPositionsData[allPlayer.Id];
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.transform.forward = seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget.forward;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.transform.forward = seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget.forward;
			Vector3 forward = allPlayer.transform.forward;
			forward.Normalize();
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.ClampViewToPos(forward);
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.ClampIntro = true;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = allPlayer.IsOwner;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetCameraTarget(seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget);
			++allPlayer.Movement.IsBusy;
		}
		_audioSource.PlayOneShot(_crashAudio);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(_explosion, _explosionPos.position, Quaternion.identity).transform.parent = _sceneContainer.transform;
		CameraSpeedEffect(-25f, 0.2f);
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer2 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer2.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(0.2f, 1f);
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.2f);
		CameraSpeedEffect(25f, 0.35f);
		_rubberDuckEasterEgg.SetActive(value: true);
		ChangeGravity(new Vector3(-3f, 3f, 10f));
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer3 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			_seatingPositionsData[allPlayer3.Id].SittingCameraTarget.localEulerAngles = new Vector3(-12f, 0f, 5f);
			allPlayer3.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(19f, 0.07f);
		}
		_toggleLightingSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(ToggleLighting());
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(9f);
		CameraTiltEffect(45f, 9f);
		ChangeGravity(new Vector3(-9f, 9f, 1f));
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(6f);
		AudioManager.GetAudioPlayer().Play2D(_endGameCrashAudio, AudioMixerChannels.FX);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(4f);
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer4 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer4.Statistics.ApplyDamage(0f);
			allPlayer4.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(1f, 0.15f);
		}
		ChangeGravity(new Vector3(3f, 0f, -12f));
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		float timer = 0f;
		while (timer < 0.5f)
		{
			foreach (IPlayer allPlayer5 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
			{
				SeatingPositionData seatingPositionData2 = _seatingPositionsData[allPlayer5.Id];
				seatingPositionData2.SittingCameraTarget.position = Vector3.Lerp(seatingPositionData2.SittingCameraTarget.position, seatingPositionData2.PlayerControllerPosition.position + seatingPositionData2.PlayerControllerPosition.forward, Time.deltaTime * 6f);
			}
			timer += Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_toggleLightingSequence);
		ResetGravity();
		yield return 0f;
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> WakeUp()
	{
		_JetAmbience.Play();
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			SeatingPositionData seatingPositionData = _seatingPositionsData[allPlayer.Id];
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.transform.forward = seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget.forward;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.transform.forward = seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget.forward;
			Vector3 forward = allPlayer.transform.forward;
			forward.Normalize();
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.ClampViewToPos(forward);
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.ClampIntro = true;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = false;
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(2f);
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer2 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer2.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(0.5f, 0.05f);
		}
		if (PlayerUtilities.GetServerCharacterGender() == EGameGenderMode.Male)
		{
			_audioSource.PlayOneShot(_wakeAudio);
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(2f);
		_audioSource.PlayOneShot(_beltWarning);
		_canSkipIntro = true;
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer3 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer3.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = allPlayer3.IsOwner;
		}
		_backLight.intensity = 0f;
		_backLights.SetActive(value: true);
		while (_backLight.intensity < 1f)
		{
			_backLight.intensity += Time.deltaTime * 0.1f;
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		_loosingAltitudeSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(LoosingAltitude());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> LoosingAltitude()
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			SeatingPositionData seatingPositionData = _seatingPositionsData[allPlayer.Id];
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.transform.position = seatingPositionData.PlayerControllerPosition.position;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.SetCameraTarget(seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget);
			++allPlayer.Movement.IsBusy;
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(8f);
		_randomTurbulence.Play();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer2 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer2.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(2.4f, 0.1f);
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(2f);
		_audioSource.PlayOneShot(_crashAudio);
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(_explosion, _explosionPos.position, Quaternion.identity).transform.parent = _sceneContainer.transform;
		CameraSpeedEffect(-25f, 0.2f);
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer3 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer3.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(0.2f, 1f);
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.2f);
		CameraSpeedEffect(25f, 0.35f);
		_jetDamaged.SetActive(value: true);
		_jetComplete.SetActive(value: false);
		ChangeGravity(new Vector3(-3f, 3f, 10f));
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer4 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			_seatingPositionsData[allPlayer4.Id].SittingCameraTarget.localEulerAngles = new Vector3(-12f, 0f, 5f);
			allPlayer4.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(19f, 0.07f);
		}
		_toggleLightingSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(ToggleLighting());
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(9f);
		CameraTiltEffect(45f, 9f);
		ChangeGravity(new Vector3(-9f, 9f, 1f));
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(10f);
		_crashImpactWaterSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(CrashImpactWater());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> CrashImpactWater()
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer.Statistics.ApplyDamage(0f);
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(1f, 0.15f);
		}
		ChangeGravity(new Vector3(3f, 0f, -12f));
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
		_canPause = false;
		Singleton<Fader>.Instance.FadeOut(0.3f);
		float timer = 0f;
		while (timer < 0.5f)
		{
			foreach (IPlayer allPlayer2 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
			{
				SeatingPositionData seatingPositionData = _seatingPositionsData[allPlayer2.Id];
				seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget.position = Vector3.Lerp(seatingPositionData.SittingCameraTarget.position, seatingPositionData.PlayerControllerPosition.position + seatingPositionData.PlayerControllerPosition.forward, Time.deltaTime * 6f);
			}
			timer += Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(3f);
		_landedInWaterSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(LandedInWater());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> LandedInWater()
	{
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_toggleLightingSequence);
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer.transform.gameObject.SetActive(value: true);
			allPlayer.transform.position = _playerCrashPosition.position;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = false;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ResetCameraTarget();
			allPlayer.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
		}
		ResetGravity();
		_jetNonCrashed.SetActive(value: false);
		_jetCrashed.SetActive(value: true);
		yield return Singleton<Fader>.Instance.FadeIn_Async(2f);
		_canPause = true;
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer2 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer2.Movement.IsBusy = default;
			allPlayer2.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = allPlayer2.IsOwner;
			allPlayer2.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.ClampIntro = false;
			allPlayer2.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(1f, 0.05f);
		}
		_landingCrashExplosionsSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(LandedCrashExplosions());
		Singleton<PrivateJetTutorial>.Instance.FindLifeRaft = true;
		float random = UnityEngine.Random.Range(0f, 2.1474836E+09f);
		bool playerNearRaft = false;
		while (!playerNearRaft)
		{
			playerNearRaft = PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers.Any_NonAlloc((IPlayer p) => Vector3.Distance(p.transform.position, new Vector3(-6.7f, 0f, 1.78f)) < 3f);
			foreach (Transform underwaterProp in _underwaterProps)
			{
				underwaterProp.Rotate(new Vector3(16f * Time.deltaTime, 8f * Time.deltaTime, 0f), Space.Self);
			}
			foreach (IPlayer allPlayer3 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
			{
				if (allPlayer3.Movement.InWater)
				{
					_jetEngine.GetComponent<AudioLowPassFilter>().enabled = !allPlayer3.Movement.isFloating;
				}
			}
			float t = Mathf.PerlinNoise(random, Time.time * 2f);
			float num = Mathf.Lerp(0f, 1f, t);
			_crashedMonitor.SetActive(num > 0.5f);
			yield return 0f;
		}
		_endSceneSequence = Timing.RunCoroutine(EndScene());
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> LandedCrashExplosions()
	{
		while (true)
		{
			yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(UnityEngine.Random.Range(5, 15));
			UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(explosion, explosionPositions[UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, explosionPositions.Length)].transform.position, Quaternion.identity).transform.parent = _sceneContainer.transform;
			foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
			{
				allPlayer.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(1.5f, 0.015f);
			}
		}
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> EndScene()
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			++allPlayer.Movement.IsBusy;
			allPlayer.PlayerCamera.MouseLook.enabled = false;
		}
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer2 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer2.PlayerCamera.CameraMovement.ShakeCamera(1f, 0.5f);
		}
		CameraSpeedEffect(25f, 0.2f);
		UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(explosion, _finalExplosionPos.position, Quaternion.identity).transform.parent = _sceneContainer.transform;
		_jetEngine.Stop();
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(0.3f);
		_canPause = false;
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer3 in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			allPlayer3.Statistics.ApplyDamage(0f);
		}
		Singleton<Fader>.Instance.FadeOut(0.25f);
		while (AudioListener.volume > 0f)
		{
			AudioListener.volume -= 0.25f * Time.deltaTime;
			yield return 0f;
		}
		yield return Timing.WaitForSeconds(1f);
		PlayerPrefs.SetInt("Completed_Tutorial", 1);
		LoadNewGame();
	}

	private void CameraSpeedEffect(float fov, float duration)
	{
		LeanTween.value(gameObject, UpdateCameraSpeedEffect, Camera.main.fieldOfView, Camera.main.fieldOfView + fov, duration);
	}

	private void UpdateCameraSpeedEffect(float fov)
	{
		Camera.main.fieldOfView = fov;
	}

	private void CameraTiltEffect(float maxTilt, float duration)
	{
		float z = _seatingPositionsData[0].SittingCameraTarget.localEulerAngles.z;
		LeanTween.value(gameObject, UpdateCameraTiltEffect, z, maxTilt, duration);
	}

	private void UpdateCameraTiltEffect(float maxTilt)
	{
		foreach (IPlayer allPlayer in PlayerRegistry.AllPlayers)
		{
			_seatingPositionsData[allPlayer.Id].SittingCameraTarget.localEulerAngles = new Vector3(-12f, 0f, maxTilt);
		}
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> ToggleLighting()
	{
		float random = UnityEngine.Random.Range(0f, 2.1474836E+09f);
		while (true)
		{
			float t = Mathf.PerlinNoise(random, Time.time * 3f);
			float num = Mathf.Lerp(0f, 1f, t);
			_backLights.SetActive(num > 0.5f);
			_monitor.SetActive(num > 0.5f);
			yield return 0f;
		}
	}

	private void ChangeGravity(Vector3 value)
	{
		Physics.gravity = value;
	}

	private void ResetGravity()
	{
		ChangeGravity(new Vector3(0f, -9.81f, 0f));
	}

	private void LoadNewGame()
	{
		MultiplayerMng.SendGameStateChangeGlobalEvent(Game.State, GameState.NEW_GAME);
	}

	private void Listener_GameStateChangeGlobalEvent(GameStateChangeGlobalEvent evnt)
	{
		if (evnt.FromState == 1 && evnt.ToState == 2)
		{
			MultiplayerMng.Log($"Listener_GameStateChangeGlobalEvent From {(GameState)evnt.FromState} To {(GameState)evnt.ToState}");
			_loadNewGame = Timing.RunCoroutine(LoadNewGame_Sequence());
		}
	}

	private void MainMenuPresenter_QuittingToMenu()
	{
		if ((bool)this)
		{
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_loadNewGame);
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_wakeUpSequence);
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_loosingAltitudeSequence);
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_toggleLightingSequence);
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_crashImpactWaterSequence);
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_landedInWaterSequence);
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_landingCrashExplosionsSequence);
			Timing.KillCoroutines(_endSceneSequence);
			ResetGravity();
			QualitySettings.pixelLightCount = _originalPixelLights;
			BeamTeam.Destroy(_sceneContainer);
		}
	}

	private IEnumerator<float> LoadNewGame_Sequence()
	{
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_wakeUpSequence);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_loosingAltitudeSequence);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_toggleLightingSequence);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_crashImpactWaterSequence);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_landedInWaterSequence);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_landingCrashExplosionsSequence);
		Timing.KillCoroutines(_endSceneSequence);
		PlayerUtilities.SetPlayerBundlesActive(active: false);
		_sceneContainer.AddComponent<DestroyPlayersOnDestroy>();
		BeamTeam.Destroy(_sceneContainer);
		yield return BeamTiming.WaitForPlayersToUnspawn();
		ResetGravity();
		QualitySettings.pixelLightCount = _originalPixelLights;
		BeamTeam.Destroy(_sceneContainer);
		Game.State = GameState.NEW_GAME;
		Singleton<Fader>.Instance.FadeOut(0f);
		UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectOfType<MainMenuPresenter>()?.LoadLevel(3);
	}
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using Beam.UI;
using Ceto;
using Funlabs;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Serialization;

namespace Beam.Ocean;

public class StructureBuoyancy : MultiplayerBehaviour, IQueryWaves
{
	private const float WATER_DENSITY = 1000f;

	private List<WaveQuery> _waveQueries = new List<WaveQuery>();

	private bool _outOfRange;

	[PREFAB_PROCESSOR_GetComponent]
	[SerializeField]
	private Rigidbody _rigidbody;

	[SerializeField]
	private BoxCollider _bouyancyCollider;

	[FormerlySerializedAs("density")]
	[SerializeField]
	private float _density = 500f;

	[FormerlySerializedAs("slicesPerAxis")]
	[SerializeField]
	private int _slicesPerAxis = 3;

	[FormerlySerializedAs("voxelsLimit")]
	[SerializeField]
	private int _voxelsLimit = 16;

	[FormerlySerializedAs("damper")]
	[SerializeField]
	private float _damper = 0.1f;

	private float _originalDamper;

	private float _voxelHalfHeight;

	private Vector3 _localArchimedesForce;

	private List<Vector3> _voxels = new List<Vector3>();

	private bool _wind = true;

	private bool _floating;

	bool IQueryWaves.Active
	{
		get
		{
			if (gameObject != null)
			{
				return gameObject.activeInHierarchy;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	IEnumerable<WaveQuery> IQueryWaves.WaveQueries => _waveQueries;

	public Rigidbody Rigidbody => _rigidbody;

	public BoxCollider BuoyancyCollider => _bouyancyCollider;

	public List<RaftBuoyancy> RaftBuoyancys { get; set; } = new List<RaftBuoyancy>();

	void IQueryWaves.InRange()
	{
		_outOfRange = false;
	}

	void IQueryWaves.OutOfRange()
	{

		}
	}

	private void Awake()
	{
		_originalDamper = _damper;
	}

	{

		Resize();
	}

	public void Resize()
	{
		Vector3 position = transform.position;
		Quaternion rotation = transform.rotation;
		transform.position = Vector3.zero;
		transform.rotation = Quaternion.identity;
		_rigidbody.velocity = Vector3.zero;
		_rigidbody.angularVelocity = Vector3.zero;
		_rigidbody.useGravity = false;

		_bouyancyCollider.size = Vector3.zero;

		{
			RaftBuoyancy raftBuoyancy = RaftBuoyancys[i];
			bounds.Encapsulate(raftBuoyancy.Collider.bounds);
		}
		_bouyancyCollider.center = bounds.center;
		_bouyancyCollider.size = bounds.size;
		_voxelHalfHeight = _bouyancyCollider.bounds.size.y / 2f;
		_voxels = SliceIntoVoxels();

		float num = _rigidbody.mass / _density;
		float y = 1000f * Mathf.Abs(Physics.gravity.y) * num;
		_localArchimedesForce = new Vector3(0f, y, 0f) / _voxels.Count;
		transform.rotation = rotation;
		transform.position = position;
		gameObject.SetActive(value: false);
		gameObject.SetActive(value: true);
		_rigidbody.useGravity = true;
		bool isOwner = entity.IsOwner;
		_rigidbody.isKinematic = !isOwner;
	}

	private void CreateWaveQueries()
	{
		_waveQueries.Clear();
		for (int i = 0; i < _voxels.Count; i++)
		{
			Vector3 vector = transform.TransformPoint(_voxels[i]);
			_waveQueries.Add(new WaveQuery(vector.x, vector.z));
		}
	}

	private void Start()
	{
		if (LevelLoader.IsLoading(gameObject))
		{
			LevelLoader.CurrentLoader.PhysicalLoading.Add(() =>
			{
				Singleton<BuoyancyManager>.Instance?.Add(this);

		}
	}

	private void OnDestroy()
	{
