	}

	public void EnableWind(bool enable)
	{
		_wind = enable;
	}

	public void SetDampening(float dampening)
	{
		_damper = dampening;
	}

	public void ResetDampening()
	{
		_damper = _originalDamper;
	}

	private List<Vector3> SliceIntoVoxels()
	{
		List<Vector3> list = new List<Vector3>(_slicesPerAxis * _slicesPerAxis);
		Bounds bounds = _bouyancyCollider.bounds;
		for (int i = 0; i < _slicesPerAxis; i++)
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < _slicesPerAxis; j++)
			{
				float x = bounds.min.x + bounds.size.x / (float)_slicesPerAxis * (0.5f + (float)i);
				float z = bounds.min.z + bounds.size.z / (float)_slicesPerAxis * (0.5f + (float)j);
				float y = bounds.min.y + bounds.size.y * 0.5f;
				Vector3 item = transform.InverseTransformPoint(new Vector3(x, y, z));
				list.Add(item);
			}
		}
		return list;
	}

	private void FixedUpdate()
	{
		if (!Physics.autoSimulation)
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < _voxels.Count; i++)
		{
			Vector3 position = _voxels[i];
			Vector3 vector = transform.TransformPoint(position);
			WaveQuery waveQuery = _waveQueries[i];
			waveQuery.posX = vector.x;
			waveQuery.posZ = vector.z;
			float height = waveQuery.result.height;
			if (vector.y - _voxelHalfHeight < height)
			{
				float num2 = (height - vector.y) / (2f * _voxelHalfHeight) + 0.5f;
				if (num2 > 7f)
				{
					num2 = 7f / (_rigidbody.mass / 40f);
				}
				else if (num2 < 0f)
				{
					num2 = 0f;
				}
				Vector3 vector2 = -_rigidbody.GetPointVelocity(vector) * _damper * _rigidbody.mass;
				vector2.x /= _voxels.Count;
				vector2.z /= _voxels.Count;
				Vector3 force = vector2 + Mathf.Sqrt(num2) * _localArchimedesForce;
				_rigidbody.AddForceAtPosition(force, vector);
				num++;
			}
		}
		if (!LevelLoader.IsLoadingScene() && !LevelLoader.IsLoading(gameObject))
		{
			_floating = num > 0;
			if (_floating && _wind)
			{
				_rigidbody.AddForce(Current.GetVelocity(), ForceMode.VelocityChange);
			}
		}
	}
}

public class SunshaftsEffect : PostProcessEffectRenderer<SunshaftsSettings>

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Beam.Utilities;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Rendering;

public class DynamicImpostor : ImpostorBase
{
	public const float SQR_MAX_DISTANCE = 62500f;

	public static float ANGLE_BIAS = 1f;

	public static float DISTANCE_BIAS = 1f;

	private Bounds _bounds;

	private ImposterTexture _imposterTexture;

	public int maxTextureSize = 256;

	public float angleTolerance = 2.5f;

	public float distanceTolerance = 15f;

	public float zOffset;

	private int _lastTextureSize;

	private Vector3 _lastCameraVector = Vector3.zero;

	private bool _forceUpdate;

	private float _maxSize;

	private Camera _renderingCamera;

	[Header("Renderables Debug")]
	private List<RenderableProxy> _renderableProxies = new List<RenderableProxy>();

	public Bounds Bounds => _bounds;

	private void Start()
	{
		_renderingCamera = DynamicImposterManager.Instance.imposterRenderingCamera;
		_lastCameraVector = Vector3.zero;
		if (!DynamicImposterManager.Instance.Imposters.Contains(this))
		{
			DynamicImposterManager.Instance.Imposters.Add(this);
		}
	}

	private void OnDestroy()
	{
		Release();
		DynamicImposterManager.Instance.Imposters.Remove(this);
	}

	public override void Release()
	{
		ReleaseProxies();
		ReleaseTexture();
		base.Release();
	}

	public override void Refresh()
	{
		base.Refresh();
		ExtractRenderersAndBounds();
	}

	public override void SetRenderables(IEnumerable<Renderer> renderers)
	{
		foreach (Renderer renderer in renderers)
		{
			CreateRenderableProxy(renderer);
		}
		Refresh();
	}

	private void CreateRenderableProxy(Renderer renderer)
	{
		Mesh mesh = renderer.GetComponent<MeshFilter>()?.sharedMesh;
		if (mesh == null)
		{
			Debug.LogError("No mesh.");
			return;
		}
		RenderableProxy renderableProxy = ImpostorManager.CreateRenderableProxy(mesh, renderer);
		renderableProxy.Renderer.gameObject.layer = DynamicImposterManager.Instance.imposterLayer;
		renderableProxy.transform.position = transform.position;
		renderableProxy.transform.rotation = transform.rotation;
		_renderableProxies.Add(renderableProxy);
	}

	private void ReleaseProxies()
	{
		using (IEnumerator<RenderableProxy> enumerator = _renderableProxies.GetEnumerator())
		{
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				ImpostorManager.ReleaseRenderableProxy(enumerator.Current);
			}
		}
		_renderableProxies.Clear();
	}

	private void ExtractRenderersAndBounds()
	{
		_bounds = default;
		using (IEnumerator<RenderableProxy> enumerator = _renderableProxies.GetEnumerator())
		{
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				RenderableProxy current = enumerator.Current;
				if (_bounds.extents == Vector3.zero)
				{
					_bounds = current.Renderer.bounds;
				}
				else
				{
					_bounds.Encapsulate(current.Renderer.bounds);
				}
			}
		}
		_maxSize = _bounds.extents.magnitude * 2f;
		transform.position = _bounds.center;
		Transform parent = transform.parent;
		transform.SetParent(null);
		transform.localScale = new Vector3(_maxSize, _maxSize, _maxSize);
		transform.SetParent(parent);
	}

	public void ForceUpdate()
	{
		_forceUpdate = true;
	}

	public void UpdateImposter(IImpostorCameraData cameraData)
	{
		bool flag = isActiveAndEnabled && Lod.Renderers[0].enabled;
		if ((!flag && !_forceUpdate) || (_forceUpdate && !flag))
		{
			return;
		}
		Vector3 lastCameraVector = _bounds.center - cameraData.Camera.transform.position;
		bool flag2 = NeedsUpdate(cameraData) || _forceUpdate;
		if (cameraData.IsVisible(_bounds))
		{
			if (flag2 || IsTextureInvalid())
			{
				FitTexture();
				Render(cameraData.Camera);
				_lastCameraVector = lastCameraVector;
				_forceUpdate = false;
			}
			return;
		}
		bool flag3 = cameraData.IsVisibleInCache(_bounds);
		if (flag3 && DynamicImposterManager.Instance.cachingBehaviour == DynamicImposterManager.CachingBehaviour.preloadAndCacheInvisibleImposters)
		{
			if ((flag2 || IsTextureInvalid()) && DynamicImposterManager.Instance.GetPreloadLock())
			{
				FitTexture();
				Render(cameraData.Camera);
				_lastCameraVector = lastCameraVector;
				_forceUpdate = false;
			}
		}
		else if ((!flag3 || DynamicImposterManager.Instance.cachingBehaviour == DynamicImposterManager.CachingBehaviour.discardInvisibleImpostors) | flag2)
		{
			ReleaseTexture();
		}
	}

	private bool NeedsUpdate(IImpostorCameraData cameraData)
	{
		Vector3 vector = _bounds.center - cameraData.Camera.transform.position;
		float num = Vector3.Angle(vector, _lastCameraVector);
		float num2 = Mathf.Abs(vector.magnitude - _lastCameraVector.magnitude);
		if (!(num > angleTolerance * ANGLE_BIAS))
		{
			return num2 > _lastCameraVector.magnitude * (distanceTolerance * DISTANCE_BIAS / 100f);
		}
		return true;
	}

	public void Render(Camera camera)
	{
		Lod.Renderers.FirstOrDefault_NonAlloc().transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(transform.position - camera.transform.position, Vector3.up);
		RenderImposter(camera);
	}

	private void RenderImposter(Camera camera)
	{
		if (_imposterTexture == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (_renderingCamera == null)
		{
			_renderingCamera = DynamicImposterManager.Instance.imposterRenderingCamera;
		}
		_renderingCamera.transform.position = camera.transform.position;
		_renderingCamera.transform.LookAt(_bounds.center);
		_renderingCamera.cullingMask = 1 << DynamicImposterManager.Instance.imposterLayer;
		Vector3 vector = camera.transform.position - _bounds.center;
		_renderingCamera.orthographic = false;
		float magnitude = (camera.transform.position - transform.position).magnitude;
		float fieldOfView = 2f * Mathf.Atan(_maxSize / (2f * magnitude)) * (180f / MathF.PI);
		_renderingCamera.fieldOfView = fieldOfView;
		_renderingCamera.nearClipPlane = vector.magnitude - _maxSize;
		_renderingCamera.farClipPlane = vector.magnitude + _maxSize;
		_renderingCamera.targetTexture = _imposterTexture.texture;
		using (IEnumerator<RenderableProxy> enumerator = _renderableProxies.GetEnumerator())
		{
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				enumerator.Current.Renderer.enabled = true;
			}
			_renderingCamera.Render();
			enumerator.Reset();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				enumerator.Current.Renderer.enabled = false;
			}
		}
		_renderingCamera.targetTexture = null;
	}

	private void FitTexture()
	{
		if (IsTextureInvalid() || _lastTextureSize != maxTextureSize)
		{
			AdjustTextureSize(maxTextureSize);
		}
		_lastTextureSize = maxTextureSize;
	}

	private void AdjustTextureSize(int size)
	{
		if (_imposterTexture == null || _imposterTexture.size != size)
		{
			if (_imposterTexture != null)
			{
				DynamicImposterManager.Instance.ReleaseRenderTexture(_imposterTexture);
				_imposterTexture = null;
			}
			_imposterTexture = DynamicImposterManager.Instance.GetRenderTexture(this, size);
			for (int i = 0; i < Lod.Renderers.Length; i++)
			{
				Renderer obj = Lod.Renderers[i];
				obj.material.mainTexture = _imposterTexture.texture;
				obj.material.SetFloat("_ZOffset", zOffset);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ReleaseTexture()
	{
		if (!IsTextureInvalid())
		{
			DynamicImposterManager.Instance.ReleaseRenderTexture(_imposterTexture);
			_imposterTexture = null;
		}
	}

	private bool IsTextureInvalid()
	{
		if (_imposterTexture != null)
		{
			return _imposterTexture.texture == null;
		}
		return true;
	}
}

namespace Beam.Rendering;

public class ImpostorManager : SerializedMonoBehaviour
{
	private const string IMPOSTOR_PREFABS_PATH = "Prefabs/StrandedObjects/Impostors";

	public const string IMPOSTOR_ASSETS_PATH = "Prefabs/Impostor Baking";

	public const string IMPOSTOR_SUFFIX = "_Impostor";

	public const int DYNAMIC_ID = -2;

	public static int BILLBOARD_DENSITY_STEP = 1;

	public static int MIN_BILLBOARD_DENSITY = 25;

	private static ImpostorManager _Instance;

	[SerializeField]
	private Transform _pooledProxyContainer;

	[SerializeField]
	private Transform _activeProxyContainer;

	[SerializeField]
	private Transform _pooledContainer;

	[SerializeField]
	private Transform _activeContainer;

	private RenderableProxy _renderableProxyPrefab;

	[Header("Impostors")]
	[SerializeField]
	private Dictionary<int, ImpostorBase> _impostorPrefabs;

	private Dictionary<int, Stack<ImpostorBase>> _impostorPools = new Dictionary<int, Stack<ImpostorBase>>();

	private Stack<RenderableProxy> _proxyPool = new Stack<RenderableProxy>();

	private void Awake()
	{
		_Instance = this;
	}

	private void Start()
	{
		EventManager.AddListener<OptionsAppliedEvent>(EventManager_OptionsApplied);
	}

	{
		EventManager.RemoveListener<OptionsAppliedEvent>(EventManager_OptionsApplied);
	}

	private void EventManager_OptionsApplied(OptionsAppliedEvent e)
	{
		int num = (int)Options.GraphicsSettings.BillboardDensity;
		if (Game.Mode.IsLocalCoop())
		{
			num = Mathf.Max(num, 1);
		}
		switch ((Quality_LOW_HIGH)num)
		{
		case Quality_LOW_HIGH.HIGH:
			BILLBOARD_DENSITY_STEP = 1;
			break;
		case Quality_LOW_HIGH.MEDIUM:
			BILLBOARD_DENSITY_STEP = 2;
			break;
		case Quality_LOW_HIGH.LOW:
			BILLBOARD_DENSITY_STEP = 3;
			break;
		}
	}

	public static RenderableProxy CreateRenderableProxy(Mesh mesh, Renderer renderer)
	{
		return _Instance?.CreateRenderableProxy_(mesh, renderer);
	}

	public static void ReleaseRenderableProxy(RenderableProxy proxy)
	{
		_Instance?.ReleaseRenderableProxy_(proxy);
	}

	private RenderableProxy CreateRenderableProxy_(Mesh mesh, Renderer renderer)
	{
		RenderableProxy renderableProxy;
		if (_proxyPool.Count > 0)
		{
			renderableProxy = _proxyPool.Pop();
		}
		else
		{
			renderableProxy = Object.Instantiate(_renderableProxyPrefab);
			renderableProxy.gameObject.layer = DynamicImposterManager.Instance.imposterLayer;
		}
		renderableProxy.transform.SetParent(_activeProxyContainer, worldPositionStays: false);
		renderableProxy.MeshFilter.sharedMesh = mesh;
		renderableProxy.Renderer.sharedMaterials = renderer.sharedMaterials;
		return renderableProxy;
	}

	private void ReleaseRenderableProxy_(RenderableProxy proxy)
	{
		proxy.transform.SetParent(_pooledProxyContainer);
		_proxyPool.Push(proxy);
	}

	public static ImpostorBase Create(int id)
	{
		return _Instance?.Pop(id);
	}

	private ImpostorBase Pop(int id)
	{
		if (id == -2 && (Options.GraphicsSettings.BillboardDensity == Quality_BillboardDensity.LOW || Game.Mode.IsLocalCoop()))
		{
			return DummyImpostor.Instance;
		}
		ImpostorBase value2;
		if (_impostorPools.TryGetValue(id, out var value) && value.Count > 0)
		{
			value2 = value.Pop();
		}
		else if (_impostorPrefabs.TryGetValue(id, out value2))
		{
			value2 = Object.Instantiate(value2);
		}
		if (value2 != null)
		{
			value2.transform.SetParent(_activeContainer, worldPositionStays: false);
			return value2;
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static void Release(ImpostorBase impostor)
	{
		_Instance?.Push(impostor);
	}

	private void Push(ImpostorBase impostor)
	{
		impostor.Release();
		impostor.transform.SetParent(_pooledContainer, worldPositionStays: false);
		impostor.transform.position = Vector3.zero;
		impostor.transform.rotation = Quaternion.identity;
		impostor.ParentInstanceId = 0;
		if (_impostorPools.TryGetValue(impostor.ParentPrefabId, out var value))
		{
			value.Push(impostor);
			return;
		}
		value = new Stack<ImpostorBase>();
		value.Push(impostor);
		_impostorPools.Add(impostor.ParentPrefabId, value);
	}
}

		_prefabPaths.Add("Prefabs/StrandedObjects/Trees/");

	public static SaveablePrefab CreatePrefabFromId(uint id, Transform parent = null)
	{
		SaveablePrefab prefab = GetPrefab(id);
		if (prefab != null)
		{
			return Object.Instantiate(prefab, parent);
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static SaveablePrefab CreatePrefabFromId(JObject prefabIdData, Transform parent = null)
	{
		return CreatePrefabFromId(ConvertLegacyPrefabId(prefabIdData));
	}

	public static SaveablePrefab GetPrefab(uint id)
	{
		if (id == 0)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Prefabs::GetPrefab:: Prefab id is not valid (0)");
			return null;
		}
		foreach (PrefabReference prefabReference in _prefabReferences)
		{
			if (prefabReference.Id == id)
			{
				if (prefabReference.Prefab == null)
				{
					prefabReference.Prefab = Resources.Load<SaveablePrefab>(prefabReference.Path);
				}
				return prefabReference.Prefab;
			}
		}
		Debug.LogError("Prefabs::GetPrefab:: Prefab id of [" + id + "] not found.");
		return null;
	}

	public static SaveablePrefab GetPrefab(JObject prefabIdData)
	{
		return GetPrefab(ConvertLegacyPrefabId(prefabIdData));
	}

	public static uint ConvertLegacyPrefabId(string prefabId)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(prefabId))
		{
			return 0u;
		}
		uint value = 0u;
		_stringToUIntIdMap.TryGetValue(prefabId, out value);
		return value;
	}

	public static uint ConvertLegacyPrefabId(JObject prefabIdData)
	{
		if (prefabIdData == null || prefabIdData.Type == JObject.EToken.NULL)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Prefabs::ConvertLegacyPrefabId:: Prefab id was null or empty.");
			return 0u;
		}
		if (prefabIdData.Type == JObject.EToken.STRING)
		{
			return ConvertLegacyPrefabId(prefabIdData.GetValue<string>());
		}
		return prefabIdData.GetValue<uint>();
	}

	public static string ConvertPrefabIdToLegacy(uint prefabId)
	{
		_uIntToStringIdMap.TryGetValue(prefabId, out var value);
		return value;
	}

	public static uint GetPrefabId(JObject data)
	{
		JObject field = data.GetField("prefab");
		if ((bool)field)
		{
			return ConvertLegacyPrefabId(field);
		}
		return 0u;
	}

	public static bool IsMultiplayerEntity(uint prefabId)
	{
		string multiplayerPrefabName;
		return TryGetMultiplayerPrefabName(prefabId, out multiplayerPrefabName);
	}

	public static Vector3 GetPosition(JObject data)
	{
		return JSerializer.Deserialize<Vector3>(data.GetField("Transform").GetField("localPosition"));
	}

	public static Vector3 GetPositionFromTransformData(JObject transformData)
	{
		return JSerializer.Deserialize<Vector3>(transformData.GetField("localPosition"));
	}

	public static MiniGuid GetReferenceId(JObject data, bool safe = true)
	{
		MiniGuid result = default;
		JObject field = data.GetField("reference");
		if (field != null)
		{
			result = field.GetValue<string>().ToMiniGuid();
		}
		if (safe && result.IsDefault())
		{
			result = MiniGuid.New();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static void SetReferenceId(JObject data, MiniGuid referenceId)
	{
		JObject field = data.GetField("reference");
		if (field == null)
		{
			data.AddField("reference", referenceId.ToString());
		}
		else
		{
			field.Data = referenceId.ToString();
		}
	}

	public static void LoadPrefabMap()
	{
		if (_stringToUIntIdMap.Count != 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		TextAsset textAsset = Resources.Load<TextAsset>("Prefabs/PrefabsLegacyMappping");
		if (textAsset == null)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Prefabs:: Error loading prefab map file.");
			return;
		}
		string[] array = textAsset.text.Split('\n');
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			string text = array[i].Trim();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				string[] array2 = text.Split(',');
				string s = array2[0];
				string text2 = array2[1];
				uint num = uint.Parse(s);
				_stringToUIntIdMap[text2] = num;
				_uIntToStringIdMap[num] = text2;
			}
		}
	}

	public static void LoadPrefabs()
	{
		LoadPrefabMap();
		_prefabReferences.Clear();
		TextAsset textAsset = Resources.Load<TextAsset>("Prefabs/Prefabs");
		if ((bool)textAsset)
		{
			JObject jObject = new JObject(textAsset.ToString());
			if (!jObject.IsNull())
			{
				foreach (JObject child in jObject.Children)
				{
					try
					{
						string value = child.GetField("Path").GetValue<string>();
						uint num = ConvertLegacyPrefabId(child.GetField("Id"));
						if (num == 0)
						{
							Debug.LogError("Prefabs:: Error loading prefab id for path [" + value + "]");
						}
						else
						{
							SaveablePrefab saveablePrefab = Resources.Load<SaveablePrefab>(value);
							if (saveablePrefab != null)
							{
								PrefabReference prefabReference = new PrefabReference();
								prefabReference.Prefab = saveablePrefab;
								prefabReference.Path = value;
								prefabReference.Id = num;
								_prefabReferences.Add(prefabReference);
							}
							else
							{
								Debug.LogError("Prefabs:: Error loading prefab from path [" + value + "]");
							}
						}
					}
					catch (JSerializerException ex)
					{
						Debug.LogError("Prefabs:: Error restoring prefab!\n" + ex);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			Debug.LogError("Prefabs:: Saved prefab data was empty!");
		}
		else
		{
			Debug.LogError("Prefabs:: Error finding prefab file.");
		}
	}

	public static void LinkMPPrefabs()
	{
		foreach (PrefabReference prefabReference in _prefabReferences)
		{
			string path = prefabReference.Path;
			uint id = prefabReference.Id;
			if (MultiplayerMng.TryGetPrefabName(path, out var multiplayerPrefabName))
			{
				_prefabsMultiplayerNames[id] = multiplayerPrefabName;
			}
		}
	}
}

using System;
using Beam.Serialization.Json;

namespace Beam.Terrain;

[Serializable]
public class Map
{
	public MapEditorData EditorData;

	public JObject ObjectData;

	public float[,] HeightmapData;

	public Map()
	{
		EditorData = new MapEditorData();
	}

	public bool IsProceduralMap()
	{
		if (EditorData == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return EditorData.Id.Contains("INTERNAL_PROCEDURAL");
	}

	public bool IsCustomMap()
	{
		if (EditorData == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return !IsProceduralMap();
	}

	private bool IsInternalMap()
	{
		if (EditorData == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return EditorData.Id.Contains("INTERNAL");
	}

	public bool IsUserMap()
	{
		if (EditorData == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return !IsInternalMap();
	}
}

using System;

namespace Beam.Terrain;

{

	public const string MAP_PREFIX = "MAP_";

	public const string MAP_OBJECT_SUFFIX = "_OBJECT";

	public static IEnumerable<Map> AllMapList => _defaultMapList.Concat(_userMapList).Concat(_remoteMaps);

	static Maps()
	{
		_defaultMapList = new List<Map>();
		_remoteMaps = new List<Map>();
		_userMapList = new List<Map>();
		Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("MONO_REFLECTION_SERIALIZER", "yes");
	}

	private static LoadingResult CreateMapFolders()
	{
		try
		{
			if (!Directory.Exists(FilePath.LOCAL_MAPS_FOLDER))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(FilePath.LOCAL_MAPS_FOLDER);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "MAP_MSG_MAP_DIR_NOT_FOUND " + ex.Message,
				Succeeded = false
			};
		}
		return new LoadingResult
		{
			Succeeded = true
		};
	}

	public static bool ValidateMapFolder(string mapPath, string mapName)
	{
		if (File.Exists(PathTools.Combine(mapPath, mapName + "_EDITOR.map")) && File.Exists(PathTools.Combine(mapPath, mapName + "_OBJECT.map")))
		{
			return File.Exists(PathTools.Combine(mapPath, mapName + "_HEIGHT.map"));
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static string GetMapFolderName(Map map)
	{
		return "MAP_" + MapTools.FormatNameForSave(map.EditorData.Id);
	}

	public static void SaveMap(Map map, string directoryPath)
	{
		string mapFolderName = GetMapFolderName(map);
		string text = PathTools.Combine(directoryPath, mapFolderName);
		if (!Directory.Exists(text))
		{
			Directory.CreateDirectory(text);
		}
		using FileStream streamEditor = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(text, mapFolderName + "_EDITOR.map"), FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite);
		using FileStream streamObject = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(text, mapFolderName + "_OBJECT.map"), FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite);
		using FileStream streamHeight = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(text, mapFolderName + "_HEIGHT.map"), FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite);
		map.Save(streamEditor, streamObject, streamHeight);
	}

	public static void Save(this Map map, Stream streamEditor, Stream streamObject, Stream streamHeight)
	{
		if (!MPExtensions.IsCompressStableValuesInitialized())
		{
			MPExtensions.InitializeCompressStableValues();
		}
		try
		{
			new BinaryFormatter().Serialize(streamEditor, map.EditorData);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::SaveMap:: Error saving map editor data: " + ex.Message);
			throw;
		}
		try
		{
			using StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(streamObject);
			streamWriter.Write(map.ObjectData.ToString());
		}
		catch (Exception ex2)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::SaveMap:: Error saving map object data: " + ex2.Message);
			throw;
		}
		try
		{
			using BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(streamHeight);
			for (int i = 0; i < 257; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 257; j++)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(map.HeightmapData[i, j].Compress());
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex3)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::SaveMap:: Error saving map heightmap data: " + ex3.Message);
			throw;
		}
	}

	public static Map LoadMap(string mapPath, string mapName, bool checkVersion = false)
	{
		using FileStream editorStream = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(mapPath, mapName + "_EDITOR.map"), FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
		using FileStream objectStream = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(mapPath, mapName + "_OBJECT.map"), FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
		using FileStream heightStream = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(mapPath, mapName + "_HEIGHT.map"), FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
		Map map = new Map();
		map.Load(editorStream, objectStream, heightStream, checkVersion);
		return map;
	}

	public static void CreateTextures(this Map map)
	{
		if (!(map?.EditorData?.LargePreviewImage != null))
		{
			Texture2D texture2D = new Texture2D(1, 1);
			texture2D.LoadImage(map.EditorData.LargePreviewImageBytes);
			texture2D.wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp;
			map.EditorData.LargePreviewImage = texture2D;
			map.EditorData.Valid = true;
			if (map.EditorData.Biome == Zone.BiomeType.CARRIER)
			{
				map.EditorData.SmallPreviewImage = Resources.Load("Icons/WorldMap_Icon_Carrier") as Texture2D;
			}
			else if (map.EditorData.Biome == Zone.BiomeType.MISSION)
			{
				map.EditorData.SmallPreviewImage = Resources.Load("Icons/WorldMap_Icon_Mission") as Texture2D;
			}
			else
			{
				map.EditorData.SmallPreviewImage = MapTools.CreateHeightmapTexture(map.HeightmapData);
			}
		}
	}

	public static void Load(this Map map, Stream editorStream, Stream objectStream, Stream heightStream, bool checkVersion, bool createTextures = true)
	{
		if (!MPExtensions.IsCompressStableValuesInitialized())
		{
			MPExtensions.InitializeCompressStableValues();
		}
		try
		{
			MapEditorData editorData = (MapEditorData)new BinaryFormatter().Deserialize(editorStream);
			map.EditorData = editorData;
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::LoadMap:: Error loading map editor data:\n\n" + ex.Message + "\n" + ex.StackTrace);
			throw;
		}
		if (checkVersion)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!SaveManager.ValidateVersionNumber(new Version(map.EditorData.GameVersionNumber)))
				{
					throw new VersionException("Map [" + map.EditorData.Name + "] is out of date and no longer supported.");
				}
			}
			catch (ArgumentNullException)
			{
				throw new VersionException("Map [" + map.EditorData.Name + "] is out of date and no longer supported.");
			}
		}
		try
		{
			using StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(objectStream);
			try
			{
				JObject jObject = new JObject(streamReader.ReadToEnd());
				PatchObjectsReferenceId(jObject);
				map.ObjectData = jObject;
			}
			catch (JSerializerException ex3)
			{
				Debug.LogError(ex3.Message);
				throw;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex4)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::LoadMap:: Error loading map [" + map.EditorData.Name + "] object data:\n\n" + ex4.Message + "\n" + ex4.StackTrace);
			throw;
		}
		try
		{
			using BinaryReader binaryReader = new BinaryReader(heightStream);
			binaryReader.BaseStream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
			float[,] array = new float[257, 257];
			for (int i = 0; i < 257; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 257; j++)
				{
					ushort value = binaryReader.ReadUInt16().ToCompressStable();
					array[i, j] = value.Decompress();
				}
			}
			map.HeightmapData = array;
		}
		catch (Exception ex5)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::LoadMap:: Error loading map heightmap data:\n\n" + ex5.Message + "\n" + ex5.StackTrace);
			throw;
		}
		if (createTextures)
		{
			map.CreateTextures();
		}
	}

	private static void PatchObjectsReferenceId(JObject objects)
	{
		if (objects == null || objects.Children == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (JObject child in objects.Children)
		{
			if (child != null)
			{
				uint prefabId = Prefabs.GetPrefabId(child);
				if (prefabId != 0 && Prefabs.GetReferenceId(child, safe: false).IsDefault())
				{
					Prefabs.SetReferenceId(child, MiniGuid.NewFrom(Prefabs.GetPosition(child), prefabId, 48879));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public static IEnumerable<Map> LoadDefaultMaps(out LoadingResult result)
	{
		IList<Map> list = LoadMaps(Directory.GetDirectories(FilePath.DEFAULT_MAPS_FOLDER), out result);
		if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
		{
			_defaultMapList.Clear();
			_defaultMapList.AddRange(list);
		}
		return _defaultMapList;
	}

	public static IEnumerable<Map> LoadLocalMaps(out LoadingResult result)
	{
		_userMapList.Clear();
		result = CreateMapFolders();
		if (!result.Succeeded)
		{
			return _userMapList;
		}
		IList<Map> list = LoadMaps(Directory.GetDirectories(FilePath.LOCAL_MAPS_FOLDER), out result);
		if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
		{
			_userMapList.AddRange(list);
		}
		return _userMapList;
	}

	public static IEnumerable<Map> LoadRemoteMaps(IEnumerable<string> paths, out LoadingResult result)
	{
		IList<Map> list = LoadMaps(paths, out result);
		if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
		{
			_remoteMaps.Clear();
			foreach (Map item in list)
			{
				item.EditorData.Source = MapEditorData.EMapSource.Community;
				_remoteMaps.Add(item);
			}
		}
		return _remoteMaps;
	}

	private static IList<Map> LoadMaps(IEnumerable<string> paths, out LoadingResult result)
	{
		result = new LoadingResult
		{
			Succeeded = true
		};
		if (paths == null)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map paths were null.");
			return null;
		}
		if (paths.Count() == 0)
		{
			Debug.LogWarning("Map paths were empty.");
			return null;
		}
		List<Map> list = new List<Map>();
		try
		{
			foreach (string path in paths)
			{
				string fileName = Path.GetFileName(path);
				if (ValidateMapFolder(path, fileName))
				{
					try
					{
						Map item = LoadMap(path, fileName, checkVersion: true);
						list.Add(item);
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						LoadingResult loadingResult = result;
						loadingResult.Message = loadingResult.Message + "MAP_MSG_ERROR_LOADING_MAP " + path + "\n\n" + ex.Message;
						result.Succeeded = false;
						Debug.LogError("Map::LoadMaps:: " + ex.Message);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					LoadingResult obj = result;
					obj.Message = obj.Message + "MAP_MSG_ERROR_LOADING_MAP_FILES_NOT_FOUND " + path + "\n ";
					result.Succeeded = false;
					Debug.LogError("Error loading map. Not all files were found: " + path);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex2)
		{
			result.Message = "MAP_MSG_ERROR_LOADING_MAPS " + ex2.Message;
			result.Succeeded = false;
			Debug.LogError("Map::LoadMaps:: " + ex2.Message);
		}
		return list;
	}

	public static void DeleteUserMap(Map map)
	{
		if (map != null)
		{
			_userMapList.Remove(map);
			DeleteMap(map);
		}
	}

	public static void DeleteMap(Map map)
	{
		if (map != null)
		{
			string text = PathTools.Combine(FilePath.LOCAL_MAPS_FOLDER, GetMapFolderName(map));
			DeleteMapFolder(new string[1] { text });
		}
	}

	public static void DeleteMapFolder(string[] folder)
	{
		try
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < folder.Length; i++)
			{
				Directory.Delete(folder[i], recursive: true);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::DeleteMapFolder:: Error deleting map folder: " + ex.Message);
		}
	}

	private static void DeleteFilesInFolder(string folder)
	{
		try
		{
			string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(folder);
			for (int i = 0; i < files.Length; i++)
			{
				File.Delete(files[i]);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Map::DeleteFilesInFolder:: Error deleting files in folder: " + ex.Message);
		}
	}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Runtime.Serialization.Formatters.Binary;
using Beam.Serialization.Json;
using Beam.Utilities;
using Funlabs;
using SharpNeatLib.Maths;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Beam.Terrain;

public static class World
{
	public struct Bundle
	{
		public Map[] Maps;

		public Vector2[] GenerationZonePositons;

		public Vector2[] GeneratedZonePoints;

		public Bundle Clear()
		{
			Maps = new Map[49];
			GenerationZonePositons = (GeneratedZonePoints = null);
			return this;
		}

		public static Bundle New()
		{
			return default(Bundle).Clear();
		}

		public void Save(Stream stream, byte[] header = null)
		{
			using GZipStream gZipStream = new GZipStream(stream, CompressionMode.Compress, leaveOpen: true);
			if (header != null && header.Length != 0)
			{
				gZipStream.Write(header, 0, header.Length);
			}
			using BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(gZipStream);
			for (int i = 0; i < 49; i++)
			{
				Map map = Maps[i];
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				using MemoryStream memoryStream2 = new MemoryStream();
				using MemoryStream memoryStream3 = new MemoryStream();
				map.Save(memoryStream, memoryStream2, memoryStream3);
				byte[] array = memoryStream.ToArray();
				byte[] array2 = memoryStream2.ToArray();
				byte[] array3 = memoryStream3.ToArray();
				binaryWriter.Write(array.Length);
				binaryWriter.Write(array2.Length);
				binaryWriter.Write(array3.Length);
				binaryWriter.Write(array);
				binaryWriter.Write(array2);
				binaryWriter.Write(array3);
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < 49; j++)
			{
				binaryWriter.Write(GenerationZonePositons[j]);
			}
			binaryWriter.Write((ushort)GeneratedZonePoints.Length);
			for (int k = 0; k < GeneratedZonePoints.Length; k++)
			{
				binaryWriter.Write(GeneratedZonePoints[k]);
			}
		}

		public void Load(Stream stream, bool createTextures, byte[] header = null)
		{
			using GZipStream gZipStream = new GZipStream(stream, CompressionMode.Decompress, leaveOpen: true);
			if (header != null && header.Length != 0)
			{
				gZipStream.Read(header, 0, header.Length);
			}
			using BinaryReader binaryReader = new BinaryReader(gZipStream);
			Maps = new Map[49];
			for (int i = 0; i < 49; i++)
			{
				Map map = new Map();
				int num = binaryReader.ReadInt32();
				int num2 = binaryReader.ReadInt32();
				int num3 = binaryReader.ReadInt32();
				byte[] buffer = new byte[num];
				byte[] buffer2 = new byte[num2];
				byte[] buffer3 = new byte[num3];
				binaryReader.Read(buffer, 0, num);
				binaryReader.Read(buffer2, 0, num2);
				binaryReader.Read(buffer3, 0, num3);
				using (MemoryStream editorStream = new MemoryStream(buffer, 0, num))
				{
					using MemoryStream objectStream = new MemoryStream(buffer2, 0, num2);
					using MemoryStream heightStream = new MemoryStream(buffer3, 0, num3);
					map.Load(editorStream, objectStream, heightStream, checkVersion: false, createTextures);
				}
				Maps[i] = map;
			}
			GenerationZonePositons = new Vector2[49];
			for (int j = 0; j < 49; j++)
			{
				GenerationZonePositons[j] = binaryReader.ReadVector2();
			}
			ushort num4 = binaryReader.ReadUInt16();
			GeneratedZonePoints = new Vector2[num4];
			for (int k = 0; k < num4; k++)
			{
				GeneratedZonePoints[k] = binaryReader.ReadVector2();
			}
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			string text = base.ToString();
			if (Maps.IsValid() && Maps.Length != 0)
			{
				int num = 0;
				Map[] maps = Maps;
				foreach (Map map in maps)
				{
					num += map.HeightmapData.GetLength(0) * map.HeightmapData.GetLength(1);
				}
				num /= Maps.Length;
				int num2 = (int)Mathf.Sqrt(num);
				text += $"\nMaps[{Maps.Length}] {num2}x{num2}";
			}
			if (GenerationZonePositons.IsValid())
			{
				text += $"\nGenerationZonePositons[{GenerationZonePositons.Length}]";
			}
			if (GeneratedZonePoints.IsValid())
			{
				text += $"\nGeneratedZonePoints[{GeneratedZonePoints.Length}]";
			}
			return text;
		}
	}

	public delegate void WorldSavedCallback(LoadingResult result);

	public const string WORLD_SEED_FILENAME = "Seed.sdd";

	private const int NUM_MISSIONS = 4;

	private static Bundle[] _worlds = new Bundle[2]
	{
		Bundle.New(),
		Bundle.New()
	};

	private static int _currentWorld = 0;

	public static Bundle CurrentWorld => _worlds[_currentWorld];

	private static Map[] _mapList
	{
		get
		{
			return _worlds[_currentWorld].Maps;
		}
		set
		{
			_worlds[_currentWorld].Maps = value;
		}
	}

	public static Map[] MapList => _mapList;

	private static Vector2[] _generationZonePositons
	{
		get
		{
			return _worlds[_currentWorld].GenerationZonePositons;
		}
		set
		{
			_worlds[_currentWorld].GenerationZonePositons = value;
		}
	}

	public static Vector2[] GenerationZonePositons => _generationZonePositons;

	private static Vector2[] _generatedZonePoints
	{
		get
		{
			return _worlds[_currentWorld].GeneratedZonePoints;
		}
		set
		{
			_worlds[_currentWorld].GeneratedZonePoints = value;
		}
	}

	public static Vector2[] GeneratedZonePoints => _generatedZonePoints;

	public static void PushWorld(Bundle world)
	{
		if (_currentWorld == 1)
		{
			Debug.LogError("World is already back-up. This is client map flow issue.");
		}
		_worlds[++_currentWorld] = world;
	}

	public static void PopWorld()
	{
		if (_currentWorld != 0)
		{
			_worlds[_currentWorld].Clear();
			_currentWorld--;
		}
	}

	private static LoadingResult CreateWorldFolders()
	{
		try
		{
			if (!Directory.Exists(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_WORLD_DIR_NOT_FOUND " + ex.Message,
				Succeeded = false
			};
		}
		return new LoadingResult
		{
			Succeeded = true
		};
	}

	public static void UpgradeLegacyWorld(out LoadingResult result)
	{
		Map[] array = new Map[25];
		result = LoadWorld_(legacy: true);
		if (result.Succeeded)
		{
			CopyLegacyMapsRefs(array);
			CreateWorld(legacy: true);
			MergeLegacyMaps(array);
			SaveWorld(null);
		}
	}

	private static void CopyLegacyMapsRefs(Map[] maps)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
			{
				int num = j * 5 + i;
				maps[num] = _mapList[num];
			}
		}
	}

	private static void MergeLegacyMaps(Map[] legacyMaps)
	{
		int num = 1;
		int num2 = 1;
		for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
			{
				Map map = legacyMaps[j * 5 + i];
				int num3 = i + num;
				int num4 = j + num2;
				_mapList[num4 * 7 + num3] = map;
			}
		}
	}

	private static LoadingResult ValidateWorldFolders(bool legacy = false)
	{
		if (!File.Exists(PathTools.Combine(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER, "Seed.sdd")))
		{
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_SEED_FILE_MISSING",
				Succeeded = false
			};
		}
		try
		{
			if (Directory.GetDirectories(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER).Length == 0)
			{
				return new LoadingResult
				{
					Message = "WORLD_MSG_EMPTY_WORLD_FOLDER",
					Succeeded = false
				};
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = ex.Message,
				Succeeded = false
			};
		}
		try
		{
			int num = (legacy ? 5 : 7);
			for (int i = 0; i < num * num; i++)
			{
				string worldMapFolderName = GetWorldMapFolderName(i, legacy);
				string text = PathTools.Combine(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER, worldMapFolderName);
				if (Directory.Exists(text))
				{
					string[] directories = Directory.GetDirectories(text);
					for (int j = 0; j < directories.Length; j++)
					{
						string fileName = Path.GetFileName(directories[j]);
						if (!Maps.ValidateMapFolder(PathTools.Combine(text, fileName), fileName))
						{
							return new LoadingResult
							{
								Message = "WORLD_MSG_MISSING_MAP_FOLDER " + fileName,
								Succeeded = false
							};
						}
					}
					continue;
				}
				return new LoadingResult
				{
					Message = "WORLD_MSG_MISSING_WORLD_FOLDER",
					Succeeded = false
				};
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex2)
		{
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = ex2.Message,
				Succeeded = false
			};
		}
		return new LoadingResult
		{
			Succeeded = true
		};
	}

	private static Map CreateProceduralMap(int index, int seed)
	{
		Map map = new Map();
		Zone.BiomeType biomeType = WorldTools.GenerateZoneBiome(Zone.BiomeType.RANDOM, StrandedWorld.WORLD_SEED - seed);
		if (index == 0)
		{
			biomeType = Zone.BiomeType.ISLAND_SMALL;
		}
		float[,] heightmapData = WorldTools.GenerateZoneHeightmap(biomeType, StrandedWorld.WORLD_SEED - seed);
		map.EditorData = new MapEditorData
		{
			Name = MapNameGenerator.GenerateRandomMapName(seed),
			Id = string.Format("{0}_{1}", "INTERNAL_PROCEDURAL", index),
			VersionNumber = 0,
			Biome = biomeType,
			Author = "Beam Team",
			Description = "MAP_DESC_PROCEDURAL"
		};
		map.ObjectData = new JObject();
		map.HeightmapData = heightmapData;
		map.EditorData.SmallPreviewImage = MapTools.CreateHeightmapTexture(map.HeightmapData);
		return map;
	}

	public static bool ReplaceMapWithProceduralMap(Map replaceMap, out Map proceduralMap)
	{
		proceduralMap = null;
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < 49; i++)
		{
			if (_mapList[i] == replaceMap)
			{
				Map map = CreateProceduralMap(i, num);
				_mapList[i] = map;
				proceduralMap = map;
				return true;
			}
			num++;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static void ReplaceMap(Map mapToReplace, Map replacingMap)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < 49; i++)
		{
			if (_mapList[i] == mapToReplace)
			{
				_mapList[i] = replacingMap;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private static int[] CreateMissionTiles()
	{
		FastRandom fastRandom = new FastRandom(StrandedWorld.WORLD_SEED);
		int upperBound = 48;
		List<int> list = new List<int>();
		int[] array = new int[4];
		for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
		{
			int num = fastRandom.Next(1, upperBound);
			while (list.Contains(num))
			{
				num = fastRandom.Next(1, upperBound);
			}
			list.Add(num);
			array[i] = num;
		}
		return array;
	}

	public static void CreateWorld(bool legacy = false)
	{
		CreateWorldZonePoints(StrandedWorld.WORLD_SEED);
		_mapList = new Map[49];
		for (int i = 0; i < _generationZonePositons.Length; i++)
		{
			int seed = i;
			Map map = CreateProceduralMap(i, seed);
			_mapList[i] = map;
		}
		if (legacy)
		{
			return;
		}
		int[] array = CreateMissionTiles();
		int num = 0;
		if (Maps.DefaultMapList.Count == 0)
		{
			Maps.LoadDefaultMaps(out var result);
			_ = result.Succeeded;
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < 49; j++)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++)
			{
				if (j == array[k])
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag && num < Maps.DefaultMapList.Count)
			{
				Map map2 = Maps.DefaultMapList[num];
				_mapList[j] = map2;
				if (map2.EditorData.Biome == Zone.BiomeType.CARRIER)
				{
					map2.EditorData.SmallPreviewImage = Resources.Load("Icons/WorldMap_Icon_Carrier") as Texture2D;
				}
				else if (map2.EditorData.Biome == Zone.BiomeType.MISSION)
				{
					map2.EditorData.SmallPreviewImage = Resources.Load("Icons/WorldMap_Icon_Mission") as Texture2D;
				}
				num++;
			}
			else
			{
				int seed2 = j;
				Map map3 = CreateProceduralMap(j, seed2);
				_mapList[j] = map3;
			}
		}
	}

	public static void CreateWorldZonePoints(int seed)
	{
		int num = 49;
		float num2 = 500f;
		float num3 = num2 * 1.25f * 7f;
		int numSamplesBeforeRejection = 30;
		_generatedZonePoints = ZonePositionGenerator.GeneratePoints(seed, num2, new Vector2(num3, num3), numSamplesBeforeRejection);
		_generationZonePositons = new Vector2[num];
		FastRandom fastRandom = new FastRandom(seed);
		List<int> list = new List<int>();
		int upperBound = _generatedZonePoints.Length;
		for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
		{
			int num4 = fastRandom.Next(0, upperBound);
			while (list.Contains(num4))
			{
				num4 = fastRandom.Next(0, upperBound);
			}
			if (i == 0)
			{
				num4 = 0;
			}
			list.Add(num4);
			_generationZonePositons[i] = _generatedZonePoints[num4];
		}
		if (_generationZonePositons.Length < num - 1)
		{
			Debug.LogError("Error: Not enough island positions");
		}
	}

	public static void SaveWorld(WorldSavedCallback callback)
	{
		LoadingResult loadingResult = CreateWorldFolders();
		if (loadingResult.Succeeded)
		{
			loadingResult = SaveWorldSeed();
		}
		if (loadingResult.Succeeded)
		{
			loadingResult = SaveWorldMaps();
		}
		if (!loadingResult.Succeeded)
		{
			DeleteWorld(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER);
			callback?.Invoke(loadingResult);
		}
		else
		{
			callback?.Invoke(loadingResult);
		}
	}

	private static void DeleteWorld(string folderPath)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(folderPath))
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			if (!Directory.Exists(folderPath))
			{
				return;
			}
			DirectoryInfo directoryInfo = new DirectoryInfo(folderPath);
			foreach (FileInfo item in directoryInfo.EnumerateFiles())
			{
				item.Delete();
			}
			foreach (DirectoryInfo item2 in directoryInfo.EnumerateDirectories())
			{
				item2.Delete(recursive: true);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception exception)
		{
			Debug.LogException(exception);
		}
	}

	private static LoadingResult SaveWorldSeed()
	{
		try
		{
			using (FileStream serializationStream = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER, "Seed.sdd"), FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite))
			{
				new BinaryFormatter().Serialize(serializationStream, StrandedWorld.LOCAL_WORLD_SEED);
			}
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_SUCCESS",
				Succeeded = true
			};
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			LoadingResult result = new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_ERROR_SAVING_WORLD_SEED " + ex.Message,
				Succeeded = false
			};
			Debug.LogError("World::SaveWorldSeed:: " + ex.Message);
			return result;
		}
	}

	private static LoadingResult SaveWorldMaps()
	{
		try
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < 49; i++)
			{
				string text = PathTools.Combine(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER, i.ToString());
				if (Directory.Exists(text))
				{
					Directory.Delete(text, recursive: true);
				}
				Directory.CreateDirectory(text);
				Maps.DeleteMapFolder(Directory.GetDirectories(text));
				Maps.SaveMap(_mapList[i], text);
			}
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_SUCCESS",
				Succeeded = true
			};
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			LoadingResult result = new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_ERROR_SAVING_WORLD " + ex.Message,
				Succeeded = false
			};
			Debug.LogError("World::SaveWorldMaps:: " + ex.Message);
			return result;
		}
	}

	public static LoadingResult LoadWorld()
	{
		return LoadWorld_();
	}

	private static LoadingResult LoadWorld_(bool legacy = false)
	{
		LoadingResult loadingResult = ValidateWorldFolders(legacy);
		if (loadingResult.Succeeded)
		{
			LoadingResult loadingResult2 = LoadWorldSeed();
			if (!loadingResult2.Succeeded)
			{
				return loadingResult2;
			}
			if (!legacy)
			{
				CreateWorldZonePoints(StrandedWorld.WORLD_SEED);
			}
			loadingResult2 = LoadWorldMaps(legacy);
			if (!loadingResult2.Succeeded)
			{
				return loadingResult2;
			}
			return new LoadingResult
			{
				Succeeded = true
			};
		}
		LoadingResult result = new LoadingResult
		{
			Message = "WORLD_MSG_ERROR_VALIDATING_WORLD_STRUCTURE " + loadingResult.Message,
			Succeeded = false
		};
		Debug.LogError("World::LoadWorld:: " + loadingResult.Message);
		return result;
	}

	public static bool CheckWorldSeedFileExists()
	{
		return File.Exists(PathTools.Combine(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER, "Seed.sdd"));
	}

	private static LoadingResult LoadWorldSeed()
	{
		try
		{
			using (FileStream serializationStream = new FileStream(PathTools.Combine(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER, "Seed.sdd"), FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read))
			{
				StrandedWorld.SetWorldSeed(((int)new BinaryFormatter().Deserialize(serializationStream)).ToString());
			}
			LoadingResult result = new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_SUCCESS",
				Succeeded = true
			};
			Debug.Log("World::LoadWorldSeed:: Success.");
			return result;
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			LoadingResult result2 = new LoadingResult
			{
				Message = "WORLD_MSG_ERROR_LOADING_WORLD_SEED " + ex.Message,
				Succeeded = false
			};
			Debug.LogError("World::LoadWorldSeed:: " + ex.Message);
			return result2;
		}
	}

	private static LoadingResult LoadWorldMaps(bool legacy = false)
	{
		LoadingResult loadingResult = new LoadingResult
		{
			Succeeded = true
		};
		try
		{
			int num = (legacy ? 5 : 7);
			_mapList = new Map[num * num];
			for (int i = 0; i < num * num; i++)
			{
				string worldMapFolderName = GetWorldMapFolderName(i, legacy);
				string text = PathTools.Combine(FilePath.WORLD_FOLDER, worldMapFolderName);
				string[] directories = Directory.GetDirectories(text);
				for (int j = 0; j < directories.Length; j++)
				{
					string fileName = Path.GetFileName(directories[j]);
					string mapPath = PathTools.Combine(text, fileName);
					try
					{
						Map map = Maps.LoadMap(mapPath, fileName, checkVersion: true);
						_mapList[i] = map;
					}
					catch (VersionException ex)
					{
						loadingResult.Message = "WORLD_MSG_MAP_VERSION_EXCEPTION";
						loadingResult.Succeeded = false;
						Debug.LogError("World::LoadWorldMaps:: " + ex.Message);
						goto end_IL_0138;
					}
					catch (Exception ex2)
					{
						LoadingResult loadingResult2 = loadingResult;
						loadingResult2.Message = loadingResult2.Message + "WORLD_MSG_ERROR_LOADING_MAP " + fileName + "\n\n" + ex2.Message;
						loadingResult.Succeeded = false;
						Debug.LogError("World::LoadWorldMaps:: " + ex2.Message);
					}
				}
				continue;
				end_IL_0138:
				break;
			}
			if (loadingResult.Succeeded)
			{
				loadingResult.Message = "WORLD_MSG_SUCCESS";
				Debug.Log("World::LoadWorldMaps:: Success.");
			}
			return loadingResult;
		}
		catch (Exception ex3)
		{
			loadingResult.Message = "WORLD_MSG_ERROR_LOADING_WORLD_MAPS " + ex3.Message;
			loadingResult.Succeeded = false;
			Debug.LogError("World::LoadWorldMaps:: " + ex3.Message);
			return loadingResult;
		}
	}

	private static string GetWorldMapFolderName(int index, bool legacy = false)
	{
		if (legacy)
		{
			int num = index % 5;
			int num2 = (index - num) / 5;
			return $"{num}_{num2}";
		}
		return index.ToString();
	}

	public static float[,] ImportHeightmapFromRawFile(string path)
	{
		float[,] array = new float[257, 257];
		int num = 66049;
		num *= 2;
		byte[] array2 = new byte[num];
		try
		{
			using FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
			try
			{
				fileStream.Read(array2, 0, num);
			}
			catch (ArgumentException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Error raw heightmap import.");
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex2)
		{
			Debug.LogError("World::ImportHeightmapFromRawFile::\n\n" + ex2.Message);
			return array;
		}
		int num2 = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < 257; i++)
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < 257; j++)
			{
				float num3 = (float)(int)array2[num2++] + (float)(int)array2[num2++] * 256f;
				array[257 - i - 1, j] = num3 / 65535f;
			}
		}
		return array;
	}
}
